/* ---------- error trap (shows problems instead of a blank page) ---------- */
window.onerror = function (msg, src, line, col) {
  try { var t = document.getElementById('trap'); t.style.display = 'block'; t.textContent = 'Something went wrong on this page: ' + msg + ' (line ' + line + '). Please refresh. If it keeps happening, call us.'; } catch (e) {}
  return false;
};

var S = { role: null, token: '', bundle: null, admin: null, view: 'home', aview: 'queue', cur: null, ctab: 'profile', preview: false, filter: 'all', formKey: '', calc: 'basic', tipsSeed: Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000) };
var B = BOOT.brand || {};
function $(s, r) { return (r || document).querySelector(s); }
function $$(s, r) { return Array.prototype.slice.call((r || document).querySelectorAll(s)); }
function esc(s) { return String(s === null || s === undefined ? '' : s).replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;'); }
function money(n) { n = Number(n || 0); var neg = n < 0; n = Math.abs(Math.round(n)); return (neg ? '-$' : '$') + n.toString().replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ','); }
function num(v) { var n = Number(String(v === undefined || v === null ? '' : v).replace(/[$,\s]/g, '')); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; }
function day(s) { return s ? String(s).slice(0, 10) : ''; }
function store(k, v) { try { if (v === undefined) return sessionStorage.getItem(k); if (v === null) sessionStorage.removeItem(k); else sessionStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { return null; } }
function toast(m) { $$('.toast').forEach(function (x) { x.remove(); }); var t = document.createElement('div'); t.className = 'toast'; t.setAttribute('role', 'status'); t.textContent = m; document.body.appendChild(t); setTimeout(function () { t.remove(); }, 4200); }
function api(action, payload) {
  return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
    if (!(window.google && google.script && google.script.run)) { reject(new Error('The portal server is not reachable from this page. Open the portal link directly.')); return; }
    var done = false;
    var timer = setTimeout(function () { if (!done) { done = true; reject(new Error('This is taking too long. Check your connection and try again. If you are signed in to several Google accounts, try a private window.')); } }, LONG_ACTIONS[action] || 45000);
    google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function (raw) {
      if (done) return; done = true; clearTimeout(timer);
      var r; try { r = JSON.parse(raw); } catch (e) { reject(new Error('Unexpected reply from the server.')); return; }
      if (r.ok) resolve(r.data); else reject(new Error(r.error || 'Something went wrong.'));
    }).withFailureHandler(function (e) { if (done) return; done = true; clearTimeout(timer); reject(new Error((e && e.message) || 'Network problem. Please try again.')); }).api(action, JSON.stringify(payload || {}), S.token || '');
  });
}
var LONG_ACTIONS = { registrySync: 200000, docRead: 200000, firmUpload: 200000, planRequest: 200000, proposalDraft: 120000, msgDraft: 90000, formSubmit: 90000, runActivity: 120000, runUpgrade: 200000, formPdf: 90000, uploadDoc: 90000 };
function busy(btn, on) { if (!btn) return; if (on) { btn.dataset.t = btn.textContent; btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Working\u2026'; } else { btn.disabled = false; if (btn.dataset.t) btn.textContent = btn.dataset.t; } }
function run(btn, action, payload, after) {
  if (S.preview && S.role === 'admin' && after !== 'adminok') { toast('Preview is read-only.'); return Promise.resolve(); }
  busy(btn, true);
  return api(action, payload).then(function (d) { busy(btn, false); return d; }, function (e) { busy(btn, false); toast(e.message); throw e; });
}
function formData(f) { var o = {}; $$('input,select,textarea', f).forEach(function (el) { if (!el.name) return; if (el.type === 'checkbox') o[el.name] = el.checked; else o[el.name] = el.value; }); return o; }
function modal(html, wide) {
  closeModal();
  var m = document.createElement('div'); m.className = 'modal'; m.id = 'modal';
  m.innerHTML = '<div class="box" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"' + (wide ? ' style="max-width:980px"' : '') + '><button class="x" aria-label="Close" data-act="closeModal">&times;</button>' + html + '</div>';
  m.addEventListener('click', function (e) { if (e.target === m) closeModal(); });
  document.body.appendChild(m);
  var f = m.querySelector('input,select,textarea,button:not(.x)'); if (f) try { f.focus(); } catch (e) {}
  return m;
}
function closeModal() { var m = $('#modal'); if (m) m.remove(); }
function logoHtml() { return B.logo ? '<img src="' + esc(B.logo) + '" alt="' + esc(B.name) + ' logo">' : monogram(); }
function monogram() { return '<svg viewBox="0 0 64 64" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="32" cy="32" r="31" fill="#FAF7F0" stroke="#C9A961" stroke-width="2"/><text x="32" y="41" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Cinzel,Georgia,serif" font-size="24" font-weight="700" fill="#1B2951">' + esc(String(B.name || 'CC').split(/\s+/).map(function (w) { return w.charAt(0); }).join('').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()) + '</text></svg>'; }
function pageUrl(page) { return (B.url || '') + '?page=' + page; }

function sky(c) { return '<defs><linearGradient id="g' + c.slice(1) + '" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="' + c + '"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#1B2951"/></linearGradient></defs><rect width="400" height="260" fill="url(#g' + c.slice(1) + ')"/>'; }
function light(x, y) { return '<rect x="' + (x - 18) + '" y="' + y + '" width="36" height="14" rx="3" fill="#FAF7F0"/><circle cx="' + (x - 8) + '" cy="' + (y + 7) + '" r="4" fill="#C9A961"/><circle cx="' + (x + 8) + '" cy="' + (y + 7) + '" r="4" fill="#C9A961"/>'; }
function film(x, y) { var s = '<rect x="' + x + '" y="' + y + '" width="400" height="34" fill="#0E1533"/>'; for (var i = 0; i < 20; i++) s += '<rect x="' + (x + 6 + i * 20) + '" y="' + (y + 5) + '" width="10" height="6" fill="#FAF7F0" opacity=".7"/><rect x="' + (x + 6 + i * 20) + '" y="' + (y + 23) + '" width="10" height="6" fill="#FAF7F0" opacity=".7"/>'; return s; }
function bulbs() { var s = ''; for (var i = 0; i < 9; i++) { var x = 30 + i * 42; var y = 60 + 14 * Math.sin(i * 0.78); s += '<circle cx="' + x + '" cy="' + (y + 6) + '" r="6" fill="#F3D27A"/>'; } return s; }
function scene(theme) { var f = SCENES[theme] || SCENES.lake; return '<svg class="scene" viewBox="0 0 400 260" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice" aria-hidden="true">' + f() + '</svg>'; }
function wrapWords(t, n) { var w = String(t || '').split(/\s+/), lines = [], cur = ''; w.forEach(function (x) { if ((cur + ' ' + x).trim().length > n) { if (cur) lines.push(cur); cur = x; } else cur = (cur + ' ' + x).trim(); }); if (cur) lines.push(cur); return lines.slice(0, 5); }
function book(title, sub) {
  var lines = wrapWords(title, 13), y = 70;
  var t = lines.map(function (l, i) { return '<text x="80" y="' + (y + i * 20) + '" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Cormorant Garamond,Georgia,serif" font-weight="700" font-size="17" fill="#1B2951">' + esc(l) + '</text>'; }).join('');
  return '<svg class="book" viewBox="0 0 160 220" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="6" y="4" width="150" height="212" rx="6" fill="#0E1533"/><rect x="0" y="0" width="150" height="212" rx="6" fill="#FAF7F0" stroke="#C9A961" stroke-width="3"/><rect x="0" y="0" width="150" height="34" rx="6" fill="#1B2951"/><text x="75" y="22" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Cinzel,Georgia,serif" font-size="10" letter-spacing="2" fill="#C9A961">' + esc(String(B.name || '').toUpperCase()) + '</text>' + t.replace(/x="80"/g, 'x="75"') + '<line x1="30" x2="120" y1="' + (y + lines.length * 20 + 2) + '" y2="' + (y + lines.length * 20 + 2) + '" stroke="#C9A961" stroke-width="2"/>' + wrapWords(sub || 'A plain-language guide', 30).slice(0, 2).map(function (l, i, a) { return '<text x="75" y="' + (200 - (a.length - 1 - i) * 11) + '" text-anchor="middle" font-family="Arial" font-size="8" fill="#5A6177">' + esc(l) + '</text>'; }).join('') + '</svg>';
}
function reelCard(items, idx) {
  var it = items[idx] || items[0];
  return '<section class="reel" aria-roledescription="carousel"><div class="copy"><div class="kick">' + esc(it.kick || 'FREE GUIDE') + '</div><h1>' + esc(it.head) + '</h1><p>' + esc(it.text) + '</p><div class="row" style="margin-top:8px"><button class="btn" data-act="' + esc(it.act) + '" data-k="' + esc(it.k || '') + '">' + esc(it.btn || 'Read the guide') + '</button></div></div><div class="art">' + scene(it.theme) + book(it.book, it.sub) + '</div>' +
    (items.length > 1 ? '<div class="ctl">' + items.map(function (x, i) { return '<button aria-label="Show slide ' + (i + 1) + '" class="' + (i === idx ? 'on' : '') + '" data-act="reelGo" data-i="' + i + '"></button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') + '</section>';
}
var REEL = { items: [], idx: 0, timer: null, el: null };
function startReel(el, items) {
  REEL.items = items; REEL.idx = 0; REEL.el = el; el.innerHTML = reelCard(items, 0);
  clearInterval(REEL.timer);
  if (items.length > 1 && !(window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches)) REEL.timer = setInterval(function () { if (!document.body.contains(REEL.el)) { clearInterval(REEL.timer); return; } REEL.idx = (REEL.idx + 1) % REEL.items.length; REEL.el.innerHTML = reelCard(REEL.items, REEL.idx); }, 7000);
}

/* =================================================================
   Endless tips feed
   ================================================================= */
function tipHtml(t) { return '<article class="tip ' + esc(t.cat === 'Wisdom' ? 'Wisdom' : '') + '"><div class="cat">' + esc(String(t.cat || 'Tip').toUpperCase()) + (t.ai ? ' \u00b7 AI DRAFT' : '') + '</div><div class="txt">' + esc(t.text) + '</div>' + (t.why ? '<div class="why">' + esc(t.why) + '</div>' : '') + '</article>'; }
function tipsFeed(host, opts) {
  var st = { offset: 0, more: true, loading: false };
  host.innerHTML = '<div class="tl"></div><div class="sentinel">Loading ideas&hellip;</div>';
  var list = $('.tl', host), sen = $('.sentinel', host);
  function load() {
    if (st.loading || !st.more) return; st.loading = true;
    api(opts.action, { tags: opts.tags || '', offset: st.offset, size: 8, seed: S.tipsSeed, allowAi: !!opts.allowAi }).then(function (d) {
      st.loading = false; st.offset = d.next; st.more = d.more;
      list.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', d.items.map(tipHtml).join(''));
      if (!d.more) { if (opts.loop && d.total > 0) { st.offset = 0; st.more = true; S.tipsSeed++; sen.textContent = 'More ideas below\u2026'; } else sen.textContent = d.total ? 'You have seen every idea for now. Check back soon.' : 'Ideas are on the way.'; }
    }, function (e) { st.loading = false; sen.innerHTML = esc(e.message) + ' <button class="btn sm navy" data-act="noop">Try again</button>'; sen.querySelector('button').onclick = load; });
  }
  if ('IntersectionObserver' in window) { var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (en) { en.forEach(function (x) { if (x.isIntersecting) load(); }); }, { rootMargin: '400px' }); io.observe(sen); }
  else { sen.innerHTML = '<button class="btn sm navy">Load more ideas</button>'; sen.querySelector('button').onclick = load; }
  load();
}

/* =================================================================
   Public face
   ================================================================= */
function shell(navHtml, body) {
  return '<header class="top"><div class="wrap"><a class="brand" href="#" data-act="home">' + logoHtml() + '<span><b>' + esc(String(B.name || '').toUpperCase()) + '</b><small>' + esc(B.tagline || '') + '</small></span></a><span class="sp"></span>' + navHtml + '</div></header>' + body + footer();
}
function footer() {
  var links = (BOOT.consents || []).map(function (c) { return '<a href="' + esc(pageUrl(c.key)) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + esc(c.title) + '</a>'; }).join('');
  return '<footer class="foot"><div class="wrap"><div class="cinzel" style="color:var(--gold)">' + esc(String(B.name || '').toUpperCase()) + '</div><div>' + esc(B.preparer || '') + ' \u00b7 ' + esc(B.address || '') + ' \u00b7 ' + esc(B.phone || '') + ' \u00b7 <a href="mailto:' + esc(B.email || '') + '">' + esc(B.email || '') + '</a></div><div class="links">' + links + '</div><div class="small">' + esc(B.publicNote || '') + ' Tips are general information, not advice for your situation. ' + esc(B.version || '') + '</div></div></footer>';
}
function renderPublic() {
  clearInterval(REEL.timer);
  var gs = BOOT.guides || [];
  var nav = '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="pubHelp">Get help</button><button class="btn sm" data-act="login">Log in</button>';
  var body = '<main><div class="wrap"><div id="reel"></div>' +
    '<h2 style="margin-top:28px">Guides for the work you do</h2><p class="muted">Plain-language guides for teachers, creatives, health professionals, sales reps, venue staff, security professionals, food entrepreneurs and homeowners.</p>' +
    '<div class="grid g3">' + gs.map(function (g) { return '<button class="gcard" data-act="guide" data-k="' + esc(g.key) + '"><div class="pic">' + scene(g.theme) + '</div><div class="bd"><span class="muted small">' + esc(g.label) + '</span><b>' + esc(g.title) + '</b><span class="small muted">' + esc(g.sub) + '</span></div></button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
    '<div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:28px;align-items:start"><section><h2>Ideas for a better life</h2><p class="muted">Tax tips, money habits, business ideas and wisdom. Keep scrolling.</p><div id="feed"></div></section>' +
    '<section><div class="card" id="helpcard"><h2>Ask us for help</h2><p class="muted small">Tell us a little. Do not include Social Security, bank or tax ID numbers.</p>' + helpForm() + '</div>' +
    '<div class="card"><h3>Already a client?</h3><p class="small">Log in with your last name and your 4-digit PIN to see your plan, tasks, net worth and forms.</p><button class="btn navy" data-act="login">Log in</button></div></section></div></div></main>';
  $('#app').innerHTML = shell(nav, body);
  startReel($('#reel'), gs.map(function (g) { return { kick: 'FREE GUIDE \u00b7 ' + g.label.toUpperCase(), head: 'Learn 10 ways to keep more of what you earn', text: g.title + ': ' + g.sub + '. Smart, lawful ways to lower your income, investing and estate taxes.', theme: g.theme, book: g.title, sub: g.sub, act: 'guide', k: g.key }; }));
  tipsFeed($('#feed'), { action: 'tips', loop: true });
}
function helpForm() {
  var ct = BOOT.consentText || {};
  return '<form data-form="pubRequest"><div class="hp" aria-hidden="true"><label>Website<input name="website" tabindex="-1" autocomplete="off"></label></div>' +
    '<label>Your name<input name="name" required autocomplete="name"></label><div class="row"><div><label>Email<input name="email" type="email" autocomplete="email"></label></div><div><label>Mobile phone<input name="phone" type="tel" autocomplete="tel"></label></div></div>' +
    '<label>How can we help?<textarea name="need" placeholder="For example: I owe the IRS, I need a tax plan, I started a business"></textarea></label>' +
    '<label>Referral code (optional)<input name="code"></label>' +
    '<label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="privacyAck" required> <span>I have read the <a href="' + esc(pageUrl('privacy')) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Privacy Notice</a>.</span></label>' +
    (ct.sms_account ? '<label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="smsConsent"> <span>' + esc(ct.sms_account.full) + ' <a href="' + esc(pageUrl('sms')) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">SMS Terms</a></span></label>' : '') +
    (ct.sms_marketing ? '<label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="smsMarketing"> <span>(Optional) ' + esc(ct.sms_marketing.full) + '</span></label>' : '') +
    (ct.email_marketing ? '<label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="emailUpdates"> <span>' + esc(ct.email_marketing.short) + '</span></label>' : '') +
    '<p class="small muted">Checking a text box is optional and not required to get help from us.</p><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Send</button><div class="msg"></div></form>';
}
function guideModal(key) {
  var g = (BOOT.guides || []).filter(function (x) { return x.key === key; })[0]; if (!g) return;
  var m = modal('<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:130px 1fr;gap:16px;align-items:center"><div style="position:relative;height:180px">' + book(g.title, g.sub).replace('class="book"', 'style="width:120px"') + '</div><div><div class="cinzel small" style="color:var(--gold2)">FREE GUIDE</div><h2>' + esc(g.title) + '</h2><p class="muted">' + esc(g.sub) + '</p></div></div><h3>10 ways to keep more of what you earn</h3><div id="gtips"></div><p class="small muted">General information, not advice for your situation. Your tax team confirms what fits you.</p><button class="btn" data-act="pubHelp">Ask us about your situation</button>', true);
  var host = $('#gtips', m);
  host.textContent = 'Loading\u2026';
  api('tips', { tags: g.tags, offset: 0, size: 10, seed: 3 }).then(function (d) { host.innerHTML = '<ol class="steps">' + d.items.map(function (t) { return '<li><b>' + esc(t.text) + '</b>' + (t.why ? '<div class="small muted">' + esc(t.why) + '</div>' : '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ol>'; }, function (e) { host.textContent = e.message; });
}
function setPinModal(r) {
  modal('<h2>Choose your own PIN</h2><p>Welcome' + (r.label ? ', ' + esc(r.label) : '') + '. You signed in with a temporary PIN. Choose your own 4-digit PIN now. You will use it every time you log in.</p>' +
    '<form data-form="setOwnPin"><input type="hidden" name="changeToken" value="' + esc(r.changeToken) + '">' +
    '<label>New 4-digit PIN<input name="newPin" type="password" inputmode="numeric" pattern="[0-9]{4}" maxlength="4" autocomplete="new-password" required></label>' +
    '<label>Type it again<input name="confirmPin" type="password" inputmode="numeric" pattern="[0-9]{4}" maxlength="4" autocomplete="new-password" required></label>' +
    '<p class="small muted">Avoid easy PINs like 1111 or 1234. Do not use any part of your Social Security number. Keep your PIN private.</p>' +
    '<button class="btn navy" type="submit">Save my PIN and open my portal</button><div class="msg"></div></form>');
}
function loginModal(tab) {
  tab = tab || 'tp';
  modal('<h2>Log in</h2><div class="tabs"><button class="' + (tab === 'tp' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-act="loginTab" data-k="tp">Taxpayer</button><button class="' + (tab === 'firm' ? 'on' : '') + '" data-act="loginTab" data-k="firm">Firm</button></div>' +
    (tab === 'tp' ? '<form data-form="clientLogin"><label>Last name<input name="lastName" autocomplete="family-name" required></label><label>Your 4-digit PIN<input name="last4" type="password" inputmode="numeric" pattern="[0-9]{4}" maxlength="4" autocomplete="off" required></label><p class="small muted">Use the PIN we sent you. New clients get a temporary PIN and choose their own the first time they log in. Only a scrambled code of your PIN is kept.</p><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Log in</button><div class="msg"></div></form>' :
      '<form data-form="adminLogin"><label>Firm PIN<input name="pin" type="password" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="current-password" required></label><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Log in</button><div class="msg"></div></form>' +
      '<details style="margin-top:14px"><summary>Team member (intern or staff)</summary><div class="in"><form data-form="staffLogin"><label>Your email<input name="email" type="email" autocomplete="username" required></label><label>Your PIN<input name="pin" type="password" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="current-password" required></label><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Log in</button><div class="msg"></div></form></div></details>') +
    '<p class="small muted" style="margin-top:14px">Trouble logging in? Call ' + esc(B.phone || '') + '.</p>');
}

/* =================================================================
   Client portal
   ================================================================= */
var CVIEWS = [['home', 'Home'], ['plan', 'Plan'], ['work', 'My work'], ['tasks', 'Tasks'], ['goals', 'Goals'], ['worth', 'Net worth'], ['forms', 'Forms'], ['docs', 'Documents'], ['messages', 'Messages'], ['calc', 'Calculators'], ['ask', 'Ask & share'], ['refer', 'Refer a friend'], ['apps', 'Apps'], ['merch', 'Merch plan'], ['tips', 'Ideas'], ['account', 'Account & logins'], ['privacy', 'Privacy & consents']];
function C() { return S.bundle; }
function cGranted(k) { var s = (C().consents || {})[k]; return !!(s && s.status === 'granted'); }
function renderClient() {
  clearInterval(REEL.timer);
  var b = C(); if (!b) return renderPublic();
  if (b.brand) B = b.brand;
  var needGate = !S.preview && (!cGranted('portal_terms') || !cGranted('esign'));
  if (S.who === undefined || S.whoFor !== b.client.id) { S.who = (b.v2 && b.v2.person) || ''; S.whoFor = b.client.id; }
  var unread = V2().messages.filter(function (m) { return m.dir === 'out' && m.status === 'sent'; }).length;
  var views = CVIEWS.filter(function (v) { return !(v[0] === 'apps' && !b.apps.length) && !(v[0] === 'merch' && !b.merch.length) && !(v[0] === 'work' && !V2().people.length); });
  var nav = '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="' + (S.preview ? 'exitPreview' : 'logout') + '">' + (S.preview ? 'Exit preview' : 'Log out') + '</button>' +
    (needGate ? '' : '<nav class="nav" aria-label="Portal sections">' + views.map(function (v) { return '<button class="' + (S.view === v[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-act="cview" data-k="' + v[0] + '">' + esc(v[1]) + (v[0] === 'messages' && unread ? '<span class="dot">' + unread + '</span>' : '') + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</nav>');
  var body = '<main><div class="wrap">' + (needGate ? gateView() : (CV[S.view] || CV.home)()) + '</div></main>';
  $('#app').innerHTML = (S.preview ? '<div class="preview">Preview of ' + esc(b.client.name) + '\'s portal (read-only)</div>' : '') + shell(nav, body);
  if (!needGate && CVA[S.view]) CVA[S.view]();
  window.scrollTo(0, 0);
}
function gateView() {
  var ct = C().consentText;
  return '<div class="card" style="max-width:780px;margin:0 auto"><div class="cinzel small" style="color:var(--gold2)">WELCOME, ' + esc(String(C().you).toUpperCase()) + '</div><h1>Before you start</h1><p>Please read and accept these two items. They let us show your plan and records here and let you sign forms in the portal. You can print them any time.</p>' +
    ['portal_terms', 'esign'].map(function (k) { return '<h3>' + esc(ct[k].title) + '</h3><div class="legal">' + esc(ct[k].full) + '</div><label class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-gate="' + k + '"' + (cGranted(k) ? ' checked disabled' : '') + '> <span>' + esc(ct[k].short) + '</span></label>'; }).join('') +
    '<div class="row"><button class="btn navy" data-act="gateOk">I agree and continue</button><a class="btn ghost" href="' + esc(pageUrl('consents')) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Print these</a></div><p class="small muted">Want paper copies instead? Call ' + esc(B.phone) + '. If you do not agree, log out; nothing is recorded.</p></div>';
}
function you() { return C().you; }
function ownerOpts(sel) { return C().client.owners.map(function (o) { return '<option' + (o === sel ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(o) + '</option>'; }).join(''); }
function statusPill(st) { var m = { 'new': ['New', ''], reviewed: ['Reviewed', 'gold'], applied: ['Applied', 'good'], dismissed: ['Not changed', 'warn'], pending: ['Waiting on team', ''], confirmed: ['Confirmed by team', 'good'], corrected: ['Corrected by team', 'warn'], awaiting: ['Waiting on team', ''], draft: ['Draft ready', 'gold'], approved: ['Approved', 'good'] }[st] || [st, '']; return '<span class="pill ' + m[1] + '">' + esc(m[0]) + '</span>'; }
function toneCls(t) { return { good: 'good', warn: 'warn', bad: 'bad' }[t] || ''; }
function netTotals(h) { var own = 0, owe = 0; h.forEach(function (x) { own += num(x.Value); owe += num(x.Owed); }); return { own: own, owe: owe, net: own - owe }; }

var CV = {}, CVA = {};
CV.home = function () {
  var b = C(), c = b.client, open = b.tasks.filter(function (t) { return t.Done !== 'Y'; }), nt = netTotals(b.holdings);
  var recs = (b.recs || []).map(function (r) { var f = formByKey(r.key); return f ? '<div class="banner"><span class="pill gold">FORM</span><span><b>' + esc(f.title) + '</b><br><span class="small muted">Your plan mentions: "' + esc(r.reason.slice(0, 120)) + '"</span></span><span class="sp"></span><button class="btn sm navy" data-act="openForm" data-k="' + esc(f.key) + '">Start</button></div>' : ''; }).join('');
  return '<div id="reel"></div>' +
    '<div class="grid g3" style="margin:18px 0"><div class="stat"><span class="small">Open tasks</span><b>' + open.length + '</b></div><div class="stat"><span class="small">Net worth (what you entered)</span><b>' + money(nt.net) + '</b></div><div class="stat"><span class="small">Plan sections</span><b>' + b.sections.length + '</b></div></div>' +
    (recs ? '<h2>Forms your plan calls for</h2>' + recs : '') +
    '<div class="grid g2"><div class="card"><h2>' + esc(c.planTitle || 'Your plan') + '</h2><p>' + esc(c.planSummary || '') + '</p>' + (c.balanceNote ? '<p class="small muted">Balance source: ' + esc(c.balanceNote) + '</p>' : '') + '<button class="btn navy" data-act="cview" data-k="plan">Open your plan</button></div>' +
    '<div class="card"><h2>Next up</h2>' + (open.slice(0, 5).map(taskRow).join('') || '<p class="muted">Nothing open. Nice work.</p>') + '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="cview" data-k="tasks">All tasks</button></div></div>' +
    homeV2() +
    '<h2>Ideas picked for you</h2><div id="hfeed"></div>';
};
CVA.home = function () {
  var c = C().client;
  var items = [{ kick: 'YOUR GUIDE', head: c.guideTitle || 'Your plan', text: c.headline || '', theme: c.theme, book: c.guideTitle || c.planTitle, sub: c.guideSub, act: 'cview', k: 'plan', btn: 'Read your plan' }];
  if ((C().recs || []).some(function (r) { return r.key === 'w4'; })) items.push({ kick: 'PAYCHECK CHECKUP', head: 'Update your W-4 in 10 minutes', text: 'Your plan calls for a withholding change. Answer a few questions, print your answer sheet and hand it to payroll.', theme: c.theme, book: 'Your W-4 Made Simple', sub: 'Step by step', act: 'openForm', k: 'w4', btn: 'Start the W-4 helper' });
  C().goals.slice().reverse().slice(0, 3).forEach(function (g) { items.push({ kick: 'YOUR GOAL' + (g.Person ? ' \u00b7 ' + String(g.Person).toUpperCase() : ''), head: g.Title, text: (g.plan && g.plan.summary) || g.Detail || 'We are building the steps for this goal.', theme: c.theme, book: g.Title, sub: 'Goal plan', act: 'cview', k: 'goals', btn: 'See the steps' }); });
  items.push({ kick: 'KNOW YOUR NUMBERS', head: 'Track what you own and what you owe', text: 'Your net worth page keeps your property, accounts and debts in one place for planning.', theme: 'lake', book: 'Net Worth Workbook', sub: 'Own, owe, net', act: 'cview', k: 'worth', btn: 'Open net worth' });
  startReel($('#reel'), items);
  tipsFeed($('#hfeed'), mineFeed({ loop: true }));
};
function mineFeed(o) { o.action = S.preview ? 'tips' : 'tipsMine'; if (S.preview) { o.tags = C().client.tags || ''; o.allowAi = false; } return o; }
function taskRow(t) {
  var lock = t.Proposed === 'Y';
  return '<div class="task' + (t.Done === 'Y' ? ' done' : '') + '"><input type="checkbox" aria-label="Done" data-act="toggleTask" data-id="' + esc(t.Id) + '"' + (t.Done === 'Y' ? ' checked' : '') + '><div class="t"><div>' + esc(t.Title) + '</div><div class="small muted">' + esc(t.Owner) + (t.Due ? ' \u00b7 due ' + esc(t.Due) : '') + (t.GoalTitle ? ' \u00b7 goal: ' + esc(t.GoalTitle) : '') + (lock ? ' \u00b7 <span class="pill">proposed</span>' : '') + '</div></div></div>';
}
CV.plan = function () {
  var b = C(), who = S.who || '';
  function secHtml(s) {
    var cs = b.corrections.filter(function (x) { return x.SectionId === s.Id; });
    return '<details' + (s.Order === '1' ? ' open' : '') + '><summary>' + esc(s.Title) + (cs.length ? ' <span class="pill">' + cs.length + ' note' + (cs.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + '</span>' : '') + (s.Person && !who ? ' <span class="pill gold">' + esc(s.Person) + '</span>' : '') + '</summary><div class="in"><p>' + esc(s.Summary) + '</p>' +
      (s.Points ? '<ul class="pts">' + s.Points.split('\n').filter(String).map(function (p) { return '<li>' + esc(p) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '') + (s.Codes ? '<div class="codes">Law: ' + esc(s.Codes) + '</div>' : '') +
      '<div class="thread">' + cs.map(corrHtml).join('') + '<form data-form="addCorrection" data-id="' + esc(s.Id) + '"><label>Something wrong or changed here? Tell us.<textarea name="text" placeholder="For example: the second property sold on May 2 for $185,000"></textarea></label><button class="btn sm navy" type="submit">Send correction</button></form></div></div></details>';
  }
  var joint = b.sections.filter(function (s) { return !s.Person; }), mine = b.sections.filter(function (s) { return who && s.Person === who; }), others = b.sections.filter(function (s) { return s.Person && s.Person !== who; });
  var secs = '';
  if (who) secs = '<div class="grp">JUST FOR ' + esc(who.toUpperCase()) + '</div>' + (mine.map(secHtml).join('') || '<p class="muted small">No individual sections for ' + esc(who) + ' yet. Your shared plan is below.</p>') + (joint.length ? '<div class="grp">SHARED HOUSEHOLD PLAN</div>' + joint.map(secHtml).join('') : '');
  else { secs = joint.map(secHtml).join(''); var names = []; others.forEach(function (s) { if (names.indexOf(s.Person) === -1) names.push(s.Person); }); names.forEach(function (n) { secs += '<div class="grp">' + esc(n.toUpperCase()) + '\'S OWN SECTIONS</div>' + others.filter(function (s) { return s.Person === n; }).map(secHtml).join(''); }); }
  var strats = b.strategies.filter(function (x) { return !who || !x.Person || x.Person === who; });
  var strat = strats.length ? '<h2 style="margin-top:22px">Strategies we checked for you</h2>' + ['Act now', 'Plan for later'].map(function (g) { var rows = strats.filter(function (x) { return x.Grp === g; }); return rows.length ? '<h3>' + esc(g) + '</h3><div class="grid g2">' + rows.map(function (x) { return '<div class="card"><span class="pill gold">' + esc(x.Num) + '</span> <b>' + esc(x.Title) + '</b>' + (x.Person ? ' <span class="pill">' + esc(x.Person) + '</span>' : '') + '<p class="small">' + esc(x.Text) + '</p><div class="codes">' + esc(x.Rule) + '</div></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : ''; }).join('') : '';
  var general = b.corrections.filter(function (x) { return !x.SectionId; });
  return '<h1>' + esc(b.client.planTitle || 'Your plan') + '</h1><p class="muted">' + esc(b.client.planSummary || '') + '</p>' + whoChips() + (V2().filingStatus ? '<p class="small muted">Filing status on file: ' + esc(V2().filingStatus) + '</p>' : '') + secs + strat +
    '<div class="card"><h3>General update</h3>' + general.map(corrHtml).join('') + '<form data-form="addCorrection" data-id=""><label>Anything else that changed (job, address, family, property)?<textarea name="text"></textarea></label><button class="btn sm navy" type="submit">Send update</button></form></div>' +
    '<p class="small muted">This plan is for planning discussion. Final tax positions are confirmed by your preparer in writing.</p>';
};
function corrHtml(x) { return '<div class="c"><b>' + esc(x.Person) + '</b> <span class="small muted">' + day(x.CreatedAt) + '</span> ' + statusPill(x.Status) + '<div>' + esc(x.Text) + '</div>' + (x.Reply ? '<div class="reply"><b>' + esc(B.signAs) + ':</b> ' + esc(x.Reply) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'; }
CV.tasks = function () {
  var b = C(), f = S.filter, people = b.client.people;
  var list = b.tasks.filter(function (t) { if (f === 'done') return t.Done === 'Y'; if (t.Done === 'Y') return false; if (f === 'mine') return S.who ? t.Owner === S.who : people.indexOf(t.Owner) > -1; if (f === 'team') return people.indexOf(t.Owner) === -1; return true; });
  list.sort(function (a, b2) { return String(a.Due || '9999').localeCompare(String(b2.Due || '9999')); });
  return '<h1>Tasks</h1>' + whoChips() + '<div class="tabs">' + [['all', 'Open'], ['mine', 'Yours'], ['team', 'Our team'], ['done', 'Done']].map(function (x) { return '<button class="' + (f === x[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-act="tfilter" data-k="' + x[0] + '">' + x[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
    '<div class="card">' + (list.map(taskRow).join('') || '<p class="muted">Nothing here.</p>') + '</div>' +
    '<div class="card"><h3>Add a task</h3><form data-form="addTask"><label>Task<input name="title" required></label><div class="row"><div><label>Who<select name="owner">' + ownerOpts(you()) + '</select></label></div><div><label>Due<input name="due" type="date"></label></div></div><p class="help">Tasks for our team show as "proposed" until we accept them.</p><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Add</button></form></div>';
};
CV.goals = function () {
  var b = C();
  return '<h1>Goals</h1><p class="muted">Tell us what you want. We draft the steps, check that they are allowed under the law, and build the tasks.</p>' +
    '<div class="card"><form data-form="addGoal"><label>Your goal<input name="title" required placeholder="For example: pay off the IRS by 2028, buy a rental, retire at 62"></label><label>Details (optional)<textarea name="detail"></textarea></label><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Build my plan</button></form>' + (b.aiOn && !cGranted('tax7216_ai') ? '<p class="small muted">Instant drafts are off. Turn on "AI-assisted drafts" in Privacy &amp; consents if you want one; otherwise our team writes it.</p>' : '') + '</div>' +
    b.goals.slice().reverse().map(function (g) {
      var p = g.plan, tasks = b.tasks.filter(function (t) { return t.GoalId === g.Id; });
      return '<div class="card"><div class="row"><h3 style="flex:1 1 auto">' + esc(g.Title) + '</h3>' + statusPill(g.Status) + '</div>' + (g.Detail ? '<p class="small muted">' + esc(g.Detail) + '</p>' : '') +
        (p ? '<p>' + esc(p.summary) + '</p><div class="banner"><span class="pill ' + toneCls(p.legality.tone) + '">' + esc(p.legality.verdict) + '</span><span class="small">' + esc(p.legality.explanation) + (p.legality.codes && p.legality.codes.length ? '<br><span class="codes">' + esc(p.legality.codes.join('; ')) + '</span>' : '') + '</span></div><div class="grid g3">' + (p.outcomes || []).map(function (o) { return '<div class="card flat" style="margin:0"><b>' + esc(o.label) + '</b><p class="small">' + esc(o.text) + '</p></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : '<p class="muted small">Our team is building this plan.</p>') +
        (g.ReviewNote ? '<div class="reply"><b>' + esc(B.signAs) + ':</b> ' + esc(g.ReviewNote) + '</div>' : '') + (tasks.length ? '<h3>Steps</h3>' + tasks.map(taskRow).join('') : '') + (g.Status !== 'approved' && p ? '<p class="small muted">Draft. Not advice until our team approves it.</p>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
};
var CATS = ['Real property', 'Vehicle', 'Retirement', 'Investments', 'Cash', 'Tax debt', 'Other debt', 'Other'];
CV.worth = function () {
  var b = C(), t = netTotals(b.holdings), max = 1;
  var by = {}; CATS.forEach(function (c) { by[c] = { v: 0, o: 0 }; });
  b.holdings.forEach(function (h) { var k = by[h.Category] ? h.Category : 'Other'; by[k].v += num(h.Value); by[k].o += num(h.Owed); });
  CATS.forEach(function (c) { max = Math.max(max, by[c].v, by[c].o); });
  return '<h1>Net worth</h1><div class="grid g3"><div class="stat"><span class="small">What you own</span><b>' + money(t.own) + '</b></div><div class="stat"><span class="small">What you owe</span><b>' + money(t.owe) + '</b></div><div class="stat"><span class="small">Net worth</span><b>' + money(t.net) + '</b></div></div>' +
    '<div class="card" style="margin-top:16px"><h3>By category</h3>' + CATS.filter(function (c) { return by[c].v || by[c].o; }).map(function (c) { return '<div style="margin:8px 0"><div class="row small"><b style="flex:1">' + esc(c) + '</b><span>' + money(by[c].v) + (by[c].o ? ' / owe ' + money(by[c].o) : '') + '</span></div><div class="bar"><i style="width:' + Math.round(by[c].v / max * 100) + '%"></i></div>' + (by[c].o ? '<div class="bar" style="margin-top:3px"><i class="neg" style="width:' + Math.round(by[c].o / max * 100) + '%"></i></div>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
    '<div class="card"><h3>Your items</h3><div class="scroll"><table class="t"><tr><th>Item</th><th>Type</th><th>Value</th><th>Owed</th><th></th></tr>' + b.holdings.map(function (h) { return '<tr><td><b>' + esc(h.Name) + '</b>' + (h.Note ? '<div class="small muted">' + esc(h.Note) + '</div>' : '') + '</td><td>' + esc(h.Category) + '</td><td>' + (h.Value ? money(h.Value) : '<span class="pill warn">add</span>') + '</td><td>' + (h.Owed ? money(h.Owed) : '-') + '</td><td><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="editHolding" data-id="' + esc(h.Id) + '">Update</button></td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table></div><button class="btn navy" data-act="editHolding" data-id="">Add an item</button></div>' +
    '<p class="small muted">Use today\'s fair market value (what it would sell for) and the payoff balance from your latest statement. These numbers are for planning and help us fill in IRS financial statements.</p>';
};
function holdingForm(h, admin) {
  h = h || {};
  return '<h2>' + (h.Id ? 'Update item' : 'Add an item') + '</h2><form data-form="saveHolding" data-id="' + esc(h.Id || '') + '"' + (admin ? ' data-admin="1"' : '') + '><label>Name<input name="name" required value="' + esc(h.Name || '') + '"></label><label>Type<select name="category">' + CATS.map(function (c) { return '<option' + (c === h.Category ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + c + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label><div class="row"><div><label>Value today ($)<input name="value" inputmode="decimal" value="' + esc(h.Value || '') + '"></label></div><div><label>Amount owed ($)<input name="owed" inputmode="decimal" value="' + esc(h.Owed || '') + '"></label></div></div><label>Note<textarea name="note">' + esc(h.Note || '') + '</textarea></label><div class="row"><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Save</button>' + (admin && h.Id ? '<button class="btn bad" type="button" data-act="removeHolding" data-id="' + esc(h.Id) + '">Remove</button>' : '') + '</div></form>';
}

/* ---------- Forms ---------- */
function formByKey(k) { var fs = (C() && C().forms) || []; for (var i = 0; i < fs.length; i++) if (fs[i].key === k) return fs[i]; return null; }
CV.forms = function () {
  var b = C();
  if (S.formKey) return formView(formByKey(S.formKey));
  var cats = []; b.forms.forEach(function (f) { if (cats.indexOf(f.cat) === -1) cats.push(f.cat); });
  var recKeys = (b.recs || []).map(function (r) { return r.key; });
  return '<h1>Forms</h1><p class="muted">Fill in plain-language questions. The portal does the math. Press Submit and it comes straight to us with a PDF copy saved in your secure folder. Never type a full Social Security, tax ID or bank account number here.</p>' +
    (recKeys.length ? '<div class="card"><h3>Your plan calls for</h3>' + recKeys.map(function (k) { var f = formByKey(k); return f ? '<button class="btn sm" style="margin:4px" data-act="openForm" data-k="' + esc(k) + '">' + esc(f.title) + '</button>' : ''; }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
    cats.map(function (cat) { return '<h2>' + esc(cat) + '</h2><div class="grid g2">' + b.forms.filter(function (f) { return f.cat === cat; }).map(function (f) { return '<div class="card"><div class="row"><b style="flex:1 1 auto">' + esc(f.title) + '</b>' + (recKeys.indexOf(f.key) > -1 ? '<span class="pill gold">in your plan</span>' : '') + '</div><p class="small">' + esc(f.what) + '</p><p class="small muted">' + esc(f.agency) + ' \u00b7 ' + ({ print: 'You print and hand it in', firm: 'We prepare the official form', online: 'Done online; we guide you', upload: 'Upload to us' }[f.mode] || '') + '</p><button class="btn sm navy" data-act="openForm" data-k="' + esc(f.key) + '">Open</button></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>'; }).join('') +
    '<h2 id="sentlist">What you sent us</h2><div class="card">' + (b.formSubs.length ? '<div class="scroll"><table class="t"><tr><th>Form</th><th>For</th><th>Sent</th><th>Status</th><th>Your copy</th></tr>' + b.formSubs.map(function (s) { return '<tr><td>' + esc(s.title) + (s.detail ? '<div class="small muted">' + esc(s.detail) + '</div>' : '') + '</td><td>' + esc(s.forPerson || s.person) + '</td><td>' + day(s.createdAt) + (s.noticeAt ? '<div class="small muted">emailed</div>' : '') + '</td><td><span class="pill' + (s.status === 'replaced' ? ' warn' : '') + '">' + esc(s.statusLabel) + '</span></td><td>' + (s.hasFile ? '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="formPdf" data-id="' + esc(s.id) + '">Download</button>' : '-') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table></div>' : '<p class="muted">Nothing yet.</p>') + '</div>';
};
function fieldHtml(fd, pre) {
  var id = 'ff_' + fd.k, v = '';
  if (fd.prefill && pre) v = pre[fd.prefill] || '';
  if (fd.type === 'info') return '<label>' + esc(fd.label) + '</label><div class="fieldinfo">' + esc(v || 'Nothing entered yet. Add items on your Net worth page.') + '</div>';
  var lab = '<label for="' + id + '">' + esc(fd.label) + (fd.req ? ' *' : '') + '</label>';
  var help = fd.help ? '<div class="help">' + esc(fd.help) + '</div>' : '';
  var attrs = ' id="' + id + '" name="' + esc(fd.k) + '"' + (fd.req ? ' required' : '') + (fd.calc ? ' data-calc="' + esc(fd.calc) + '"' : '') + (fd.prefill && /^person/.test(fd.prefill) ? ' data-pf="' + esc(fd.prefill) + '"' : '');
  if (fd.type === 'person') { var ppl = V2().people, cur = (pre && pre.person) || S.who || (ppl.length === 1 ? ppl[0].Name : ''); return lab + '<select' + attrs + ' data-person="1"><option value="">Choose\u2026</option>' + ppl.map(function (x) { return '<option value="' + esc(x.Name) + '"' + (x.Name === cur ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(x.FullName || x.Name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' + help; }
  if (fd.type === 'select') return lab + '<select' + attrs + '><option value="">Choose\u2026</option>' + fd.options.map(function (o) { return '<option>' + esc(o) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' + help;
  if (fd.type === 'textarea') return lab + '<textarea' + attrs + '>' + esc(v) + '</textarea>' + help;
  var type = fd.type === 'date' ? 'date' : 'text', im = (fd.type === 'money' || fd.type === 'number') ? ' inputmode="decimal"' : '';
  return lab + '<input type="' + type + '"' + im + attrs + ' value="' + esc(v) + '"' + (fd.min !== undefined ? ' data-min="' + fd.min + '"' : '') + (fd.max !== undefined ? ' data-max="' + fd.max + '"' : '') + '>' + help;
}
function formView(f) {
  if (!f) { S.formKey = ''; return CV.forms(); }
  var pre = C().prefill || {};
  var off = (f.official || []).map(function (o) { return '<a class="btn sm ghost" href="' + esc(o.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + esc(o.label) + '</a>'; }).join(' ');
  var head = '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="formsHome">&larr; All forms</button><h1>' + esc(f.title) + '</h1><p>' + esc(f.what) + '</p>' + (off ? '<div class="row" style="gap:8px">' + off + '</div>' : '') + '<div class="card" style="margin-top:14px"><h3>How it works</h3><ol class="steps">' + f.steps.map(function (s) { return '<li>' + esc(s) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ol></div>';
  if (f.key === 'bizreg') head += '<div class="card"><h3>State links</h3><ul class="pts">' + (C().stateLinks || []).map(function (l) { return '<li><b>' + esc(l.st) + ':</b> <a href="' + esc(l.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + esc(l.name) + '</a></li>'; }).join('') + '</ul><p class="small muted">Agencies move their pages. If a link does not open, search the agency name.</p></div>';
  if (f.mode === 'upload') return head + '<div class="card"><form data-form="upload">' + (V2().people.length > 1 ? fieldHtml({ k: 'forPerson', label: 'Whose document is it?', type: 'person' }, pre) : '') + f.fields.map(function (fd) { return fieldHtml(fd, pre); }).join('') + '<label for="upfile">File (PDF, JPG or PNG, under 10 MB)</label><input type="file" id="upfile" accept="application/pdf,image/jpeg,image/png,image/heic,image/webp" required><button class="btn navy" type="submit" style="margin-top:12px">Upload</button></form></div>';
  var sign = f.sign ? '<div class="card flat" style="background:var(--cream)"><h3>Sign</h3>' + (cGranted('esign') ? '' : '<div class="err">Accept Electronic Records &amp; Signatures on the Privacy &amp; consents page first.</div>') + '<label>Type your full legal name<input name="signedName" autocomplete="name"></label><label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="signAgree"> <span>I confirm these answers are true and complete to the best of my knowledge, and I authorize ' + esc(B.name) + ' to prepare the official form from them. Typing my name is my electronic signature.</span></label></div>' : '';
  return head + '<div class="card"><form data-form="formSubmit" data-k="' + esc(f.key) + '">' + f.fields.map(function (fd) { return fieldHtml(fd, pre); }).join('') + sign + (f.notes ? '<p class="small muted">' + esc(f.notes) + '</p>' : '') + '<div class="row" style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Submit to ' + esc(B.name) + '</button></div><div class="msg"></div></form></div>';
}
var CALCS = {
  w4extra: function (v) { var g = num(v.gapTax), p = num(v.paychecksLeft); return g > 0 && p > 0 ? Math.ceil(g / p) : ''; },
  w4rtax: function (v) { var a = num(v.amount), r = num(v.rate); return a > 0 && r >= 0 ? Math.round(a * r / 100) : ''; },
  payoffMonths: function (v) { var b = num(v.balance) - num(v.lump), m = num(v.monthly); return b > 0 && m > 0 ? Math.ceil(b / m) : ''; },
  leftover: function (v) { var inc = num(v.incWages) + num(v.incOther); var ex = ['exHousing', 'exCarPay', 'exCarOp', 'exFood', 'exHealth', 'exTaxes', 'exOther'].reduce(function (s, k) { return s + num(v[k]); }, 0); return (inc || ex) ? Math.round(inc - ex) : ''; }
};
function recalc(form) {
  var v = formData(form);
  $$('[data-calc]', form).forEach(function (el) { if (el.dataset.touched) return; var fn = CALCS[el.dataset.calc]; if (fn) { var r = fn(v); el.value = r === '' ? '' : String(r); } });
}
CVA.forms = function () { var f = $('form[data-form="formSubmit"]'); if (f) { recalc(f); fillPerson(f, false); f.addEventListener('input', function (e) { if (e.target.dataset.calc) e.target.dataset.touched = '1'; recalc(f); }); f.addEventListener('change', function (e) { if (e.target.dataset.person) fillPerson(f, true); }); } };
function printModal(html) {

A copy is already in your secure folder.</p><div class="row"><button class="btn navy" data-act="printFrame">Print</button></div><iframe id="pframe" title="Answer sheet" style="width:100%;height:60vh;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:#fff;margin-top:10px"></iframe>', true);
  var fr = $('#pframe', m); fr.srcdoc = html;
}

/* ---------- Calculators ---------- */
CV.calc = function () {
  var tabs = [['basic', 'Basic math'], ['w4', 'W-4 extra withholding'], ['est', 'Estimated tax safe harbor'], ['gain', 'Property sale gain'], ['miles', 'Mileage'], ['se', 'Self-employment tax'], ['pay', 'Payoff time']];
  var k = S.calc;
  var body = {
    basic: '<label>Type a calculation (+ - * / ^ % and parentheses)<input id="cx" placeholder="(185000 - 140000) * 0.15" inputmode="text" autocomplete="off"></label>',
    w4: field('gap', 'Federal tax still to cover this year ($)') + field('chk', 'Paychecks left this year') + field('cur', 'Current extra withholding per paycheck ($), if any'),
    est: field('prior', 'Last year\'s total federal tax ($)') + field('agi', 'Last year\'s AGI ($)') + field('cur', 'This year\'s expected tax ($)') + field('wh', 'Withholding expected this year ($)') + '<label>Filing status<select id="c_mfs"><option value="">Any status except married filing separately</option><option value="1">Married filing separately</option></select></label>',
    gain: field('sale', 'Sale price ($)') + field('costs', 'Selling costs: commission, closing ($)') + field('buy', 'Purchase price ($)') + field('imp', 'Improvements ($)') + field('rate', 'Tax rate to estimate (%)', '15') + '<label>Held over one year?<select id="c_long"><option value="1">Yes (long-term)</option><option value="">No (short-term, taxed as ordinary income)</option></select></label>',
    miles: field('mi', 'Business miles') + field('rate', 'IRS standard mileage rate ($ per mile; check the current year)', '0.70'),
    se: field('profit', 'Net self-employment profit ($)') + field('wages', 'W-2 wages that already paid Social Security ($)', '0') + field('base', 'Social Security wage base for the year ($; confirm)', '184500'),
    pay: field('bal', 'Balance ($)') + field('mo', 'Monthly payment ($)') + field('apr', 'Yearly interest rate (%)', '7')
  }[k];
  return '<h1>Calculators</h1><p class="muted">Quick math for planning. Your tax team confirms final numbers.</p><div class="tabs">' + tabs.map(function (t) { return '<button class="' + (k === t[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-act="calcTab" data-k="' + t[0] + '">' + t[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="card" id="calcCard">' + body + '<div class="calcout" id="cout" aria-live="polite">Enter numbers above.</div></div>';
};
function field(id, label, val) { return '<label for="c_' + id + '">' + esc(label) + '</label><input id="c_' + id + '" inputmode="decimal" value="' + esc(val || '') + '">'; }
function cv(id) { var el = $('#c_' + id); return el ? num(el.value) : 0; }
CVA.calc = function () {
  var card = $('#calcCard'), out = $('#cout');
  function go() {
    var k = S.calc, r = '';
    try {
      if (k === 'basic') { var x = $('#cx').value.trim(); r = x ? '<b>' + esc(fmtNum(calcExpr(x))) + '</b>' : 'Enter a calculation.'; }
      if (k === 'w4') { var per = cv('chk') > 0 ? Math.ceil(cv('gap') / cv('chk')) : 0; r = per ? 'Add <b>' + money(per) + '</b> per paycheck in Step 4(c)' + (cv('cur') ? ' (new total ' + money(per + cv('cur')) + ')' : '') + '. <button class="btn sm" data-act="openForm" data-k="w4">Use this in the W-4 helper</button>' : 'Enter the tax to cover and paychecks left.'; }
      if (k === 'est') { var hi = cv('agi') > ($('#c_mfs').value ? 75000 : 150000); var a = cv('prior') * (hi ? 1.1 : 1), bb = cv('cur') * 0.9, need = Math.min(a || Infinity, bb || Infinity); if (!isFinite(need)) need = 0; var gap = Math.max(0, need - cv('wh')); r = 'Safe-harbor target: <b>' + money(need) + '</b> (the smaller of ' + (hi ? '110%' : '100%') + ' of last year\'s tax and 90% of this year\'s).<br>Still to pay through withholding or estimates: <b>' + money(gap) + '</b> (about ' + money(gap / 4) + ' a quarter). IRC 6654(d).'; }
      if (k === 'gain') { var basis = cv('buy') + cv('imp'), amt = cv('sale') - cv('costs'), g = amt - basis; r = 'Amount realized ' + money(amt) + ' - basis ' + money(basis) + ' = gain <b>' + money(g) + '</b>' + (g > 0 ? '<br>Estimated federal tax at ' + cv('rate') + '%: <b>' + money(g * cv('rate') / 100) + '</b>' + ($('#c_long').value ? '' : ' (short-term gains use your ordinary rate)') + '. Net investment income tax (3.8%) or state tax may add to this.' : ''); }
      if (k === 'miles') { r = cv('mi') ? 'Deduction: <b>' + money(cv('mi') * cv('rate')) + '</b>. A log with date, place, purpose and miles is required (IRC 274(d)).' : 'Enter your business miles.'; }
      if (k === 'se') { var net = cv('profit') * 0.9235, ss = Math.max(0, Math.min(net, cv('base') - cv('wages'))) * 0.124, mc = net * 0.029, tot = net > 400 ? ss + mc : 0; r = 'Self-employment tax: <b>' + money(tot) + '</b> (Social Security ' + money(ss) + ' + Medicare ' + money(mc) + '). Half (' + money(tot / 2) + ') is deductible. Additional 0.9% Medicare tax may apply at higher incomes. IRC 1401-1402.'; }
      if (k === 'pay') { var bal = cv('bal'), mo = cv('mo'), i = cv('apr') / 1200, n = 0, tot2 = 0; if (bal > 0 && mo > 0) { while (bal > 0 && n < 600) { bal = bal * (1 + i) - mo; tot2 += mo; n++; } r = n >= 600 ? 'The payment does not cover the interest. Raise the monthly amount.' : 'About <b>' + n + ' months</b> (' + (n / 12).toFixed(1) + ' years). Total paid about ' + money(tot2 + Math.min(0, bal)) + '. IRS balances also add penalties; this is an estimate.'; } else r = 'Enter a balance and a monthly payment.'; }
    } catch (e) { r = esc(e.message); }
    out.innerHTML = r;
  }
  card.addEventListener('input', go); card.addEventListener('change', go); go();
};
function fmtNum(n) { if (!isFinite(n)) throw new Error('That does not work out to a number.'); var s = (Math.round(n * 1e6) / 1e6).toString(); var p = s.split('.'); p[0] = p[0].replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ','); return p.join('.'); }
function calcExpr(src) { /* safe parser: numbers, + - * / ^ % ( ). Never uses eval. */
  var toks = String(src).replace(/[$,\s]/g, '').match(/\d*\.?\d+(?:e[+\-]?\d+)?|[+\-*/^%()x]/gi);
  if (!toks || toks.join('') !== String(src).replace(/[$,\s]/g, '')) throw new Error('Use only numbers and + - * / ^ % ( ).');
  var i = 0;
  function peek() { return toks[i]; } function next() { return toks[i++]; }
  function expr() { var v = term(); while (peek() === '+' || peek() === '-') { var o = next(), r = term(); v = o === '+' ? v + r : v - r; } return v; }
  function term() { var v = power(); while (peek() === '*' || peek() === '/' || peek() === 'x' || peek() === 'X') { var o = next(), r = power(); if ((o === '/') && r === 0) throw new Error('Cannot divide by zero.'); v = o === '/' ? v / r : v * r; } return v; }
  function power() { var v = unary(); if (peek() === '^') { next(); v = Math.pow(v, power()); } return v; }
  function unary() { if (peek() === '-') { next(); return -unary(); } if (peek() === '+') { next(); return unary(); } return postfix(); }
  function postfix() { var v = atom(); while (peek() === '%') { next(); v = v / 100; } return v; }
  function atom() { var t = next(); if (t === '(') { var v = expr(); if (next() !== ')') throw new Error('Missing ).'); return v; } var n = Number(t); if (t === undefined || isNaN(n)) throw new Error('Check the calculation.'); return n; }
  var val = expr(); if (i < toks.length) throw new Error('Check the calculation.'); return val;
}

'<label>Mobile number<input name="detail" type="tel" required></label>' : '') + (t.needsDetail ? '<label>' + esc(t.needsDetail) + '<input name="detail" required></label>' : '') + '<label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="agree" required> <span>' + esc(t.short) + '</span></label>' + (t.signature ? '<label>Type your full legal name to sign<input name="signedName" required autocomplete="name"></label><p class="small muted">Signed electronically in the portal on ' + new Date().toLocaleDateString() + '.</p>' : '') + '<div class="row"><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Agree</button><button class="btn ghost" type="button" data-act="consentDecline" data-k="' + esc(k) + '">No thanks</button><a class="btn ghost" href="' + esc(pageUrl(k.indexOf('tax7216') === 0 ? '7216' : (k.indexOf('sms') === 0 ? 'sms' : 'consents'))) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Print</a></div></form>'));
}

var AVIEWS = [['queue', 'Queue'], ['clients', 'Clients'], ['leads', 'Leads'], ['tips', 'Tips'], ['team', 'Team'], ['settings', 'Settings'], ['audit', 'Audit']];
var AVIEW_PERM = { queue: 'queue', clients: 'clients', leads: 'leads', tips: 'tips', team: 'OWNER', settings: 'OWNER', audit: 'OWNER' };
function A() { return S.admin; }
function qCount() { var q = A().queue; return q.corrections.length + q.goals.length + q.posts.length + q.referrals.length + (q.forms || []).length + (q.messages || []).length + (q.proposals || []).length; }
function renderAdmin() {
  clearInterval(REEL.timer);
  if (A().brand) B = A().brand;
  var vis = AVIEWS.filter(function (v) { return canView(v[0]); }); if (!canView(S.aview)) S.aview = vis.length ? vis[0][0] : 'queue';
  var nav = '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="logout">Log out</button>' + (ME().owner ? '' : '<span class="small" style="opacity:.85">' + esc(ME().name) + '</span>') + '<nav class="nav">' + vis.map(function (v) { return '<button class="' + (S.aview === v[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-act="aview" data-k="' + v[0] + '">' + v[1] + (v[0] === 'queue' && qCount() ? '<span class="dot">' + qCount() + '</span>' : '') + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</nav>';
  $('#app').innerHTML = shell(nav, '<main><div class="wrap">' + (AV[S.aview] || AV.queue)() + '</div></main>');
  if (AVA[S.aview]) AVA[S.aview]();
}
function cname(id) { var c = A().clients.filter(function (x) { return x.id === id; })[0]; return c ? c.name : (id || 'Public'); }
var AV = {}, AVA = {};

  function sec(t, arr, fn) { return '<h2>' + t + ' <span class="pill">' + arr.length + '</span></h2>' + (arr.length ? arr.map(fn).join('') : '<p class="muted">Nothing waiting.</p>'); }

'<a class="btn sm" href="#prop-' + esc(hasProposal(x.Id)) + '">Plan update ready above</a>' : '<button class="btn sm" type="button" data-act="proposalDraft" data-id="' + esc(x.Id) + '">Draft the plan update + reply</button>') + '<button class="btn sm ghost" type="button" data-act="openClient" data-id="' + esc(x.ClientId) + '" data-tab="sections">Edit plan by hand</button></div></form></div>'; }) +
    sec('Goals to review', q.goals, function (g) { var p = g.plan; return '<div class="card"><div class="small muted">' + esc(cname(g.ClientId)) + ' \u00b7 ' + esc(g.Person) + ' \u00b7 ' + statusPill(g.Status) + '</div><h3>' + esc(g.Title) + '</h3>' + (g.Detail ? Write the plan in the note and add tasks in the client editor.</p>') + '<form data-form="approveGoal" data-id="' + esc(g.Id) + '"><label>Note to client<textarea name="note"></textarea></label><div class="row"><button class="btn sm navy" type="submit">Approve</button><button class="btn sm bad" type="button" data-act="removeGoal" data-id="' + esc(g.Id) + '">Remove</button></div></form></div>'; }) +
    sec('Questions to check', q.posts, function (p) { var a = p.ai; return '<div class="card"><div class="small muted">' + esc(cname(p.ClientId)) + ' \u00b7 ' + esc(p.Person) + ' \u00b7 ' + esc(p.Kind) + '</div><p><b>' + esc(p.Text) + '</b></p>' + (a ? Write the answer below.</p>') + '<form data-form="reviewPost" data-id="' + esc(p.Id) + '"><label>Your answer or correction (shown to the client)<textarea name="note"></textarea></label><label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="shareAnon"> <span>Share this Q&amp;A without names in everyone\'s Ideas feed</span></label><div class="row"><select name="status"><option value="confirmed">Confirm draft</option><option value="corrected">Corrected</option></select><button class="btn sm navy" type="submit">Save</button><button class="btn sm bad" type="button" data-act="removePost" data-id="' + esc(p.Id) + '">Remove</button></div></form></div>'; }) +

};

' \u00b7 <span class="pill bad">inactive</span>' : '') + '</div></div></button>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
};
var SHEET_FIELDS = {
  Clients: ['DisplayName', 'Household', 'People', 'Occupation', 'Theme', 'Headline', 'GuideTitle', 'GuideSub', 'PlanTitle', 'PlanSummary', 'ThirdParty', 'ReferralCode', 'ReferralOffer', 'BalanceNote', 'Tags', 'Status', 'FilingStatus', 'SplitAdvice'],
  Sections: ['Order', 'Title', 'Summary', 'Points', 'Codes', 'Visible', 'Person'],
  Strategies: ['Num', 'Title', 'Grp', 'Text', 'Rule', 'Visible', 'Person'],
  Tasks: ['Title', 'Owner', 'Due', 'Done', 'Proposed', 'GoalTitle', 'Source'],
  Apps: ['Title', 'Url', 'Description', 'Order', 'Visible', 'Person', 'Kind', 'Category'],
  Merch: ['Title', 'Summary', 'Items', 'SourceDocId', 'Visible'],
  Notes: ['Note'],
  Tips: ['Tags', 'Category', 'Text', 'Why', 'Source', 'Active']
};
var LONG = ['Summary', 'Points', 'PlanSummary', 'Text', 'Items', 'Note', 'Description', 'Headline', 'Why'];
var HINT = { Person: 'Blank = whole household. Or one first name from People: then it shows on that person\'s own plan', FilingStatus: 'For example Married filing jointly', SplitAdvice: 'Y = household asked for advice per person', Kind: 'command center, app, website, document, pdf, sheet or folder (command centers show first)', Category: 'Line of work it belongs to, for example Crispus Mundo Tax, Crisp Cares Heating & Cooling, CMCC Training Institute, Nonprofit & community (501(c)(3)), Personal & family. Same name = same group', People: 'First names, comma separated (they appear as task owners)', Theme: 'school, studio, clinic, garden, workshop, arena, security, food, lake, hvac, lab, kitchen, retail, taxdesk, dental or reentry', Tags: 'Comma separated, picks tips (for example teacher, food, retirement)', Points: 'One per line', Visible: 'Y or N', Done: 'Y when done', Proposed: 'Y = waiting for firm OK', Grp: 'Act now or Plan for later', Status: 'Active or Inactive', SourceDocId: 'Google Doc ID; the portal re-reads it every morning', Url: 'Full https:// link', Active: 'Y or N', ThirdParty: 'For example: Eric Schacher, EA (Tax Resolution DRS)' };
function rowForm(sheet, row, clientId) {
  row = row || {};
  return '<h2>' + (row.Id ? 'Edit' : 'Add') + ' ' + esc(sheet === 'Tips' ? 'tip' : sheet.replace(/s$/, '').toLowerCase()) + '</h2><form data-form="saveRow" data-sheet="' + esc(sheet) + '" data-id="' + esc(row.Id || '') + '" data-c="' + esc(clientId || '') + '">' +
    SHEET_FIELDS[sheet].map(function (f) { var v = row[f] === undefined ? (f === 'Visible' || f === 'Active' ? 'Y' : '') : row[f]; return '<label>' + esc(f) + '</label>' + (LONG.indexOf(f) > -1 ? '<textarea name="' + f + '" rows="' + (f === 'Points' ? 8 : 4) + '">' + esc(v) + '</textarea>' : (f === 'Owner' && clientId ? '<select name="Owner">' + clientOwners().map(function (o) { return '<option' + (o === v ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(o) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select>' : '<input name="' + f + '" value="' + esc(v) + '"' + (f === 'Due' ? ' type="date"' : '') + '>')) + (HINT[f] ? '<div class="help">' + esc(HINT[f]) + '</div>' : ''); }).join('') +
    (sheet === 'Clients' && !row.Id ? '<label>Short ID (optional)</label><input name="NewId" placeholder="for example smith">' : '') +
    '<div class="row" style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Save</button>' + (row.Id && sheet !== 'Clients' ? '<button class="btn bad" type="button" data-act="deleteRow" data-sheet="' + esc(sheet) + '" data-id="' + esc(row.Id) + '">Delete</button>' : '') + '</div></form>';
}
function clientOwners() { var c = S.cur.client; var o = String(c.People || '').split(',').map(function (s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(String); o.push('Cyars'); if (c.ThirdParty) o.push(String(c.ThirdParty).split('(')[0].split(',')[0].trim()); return o; }
var CTABS = [['profile', 'Profile'], ['people', 'People'], ['logins', 'Logins'], ['updates', 'Plan updates'], ['sections', 'Plan'], ['strategies', 'Strategies'], ['tasks', 'Tasks'], ['holdings', 'Net worth'], ['forms', 'Forms'], ['activity', 'Goals & Q&A'], ['referrals', 'Referrals'], ['apps', 'Apps'], ['merch', 'Merch'], ['consents', 'Consents'], ['docs', 'Documents'], ['messages', 'Messages'], ['log', 'Activity log'], ['notes', 'Notes (firm only)']];
function clientEditor() {
  var d = S.cur, c = d.client, tab = S.ctab;
  var head = '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="clientsHome">&larr; All clients</button><div class="row"><h1 style="flex:1 1 auto">' + esc(c.DisplayName) + '</h1><button class="btn sm" data-act="previewClient" data-id="' + esc(c.Id) + '">Preview as client</button>' + (d.folderUrl ? '<a class="btn sm ghost" href="' + esc(d.folderUrl) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Drive folder</a>' : '') + '</div><div class="tabs">' + CTABS.map(function (t) { return '<button class="' + (tab === t[0] ? 'on' : '') + '" data-act="ctab" data-k="' + t[0] + '">' + t[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  function list(sheet, rows, title, sub) { return '<div class="card"><div class="row"><h3 style="flex:1 1 auto">' + title + '</h3><button class="btn sm navy" data-act="editRow" data-sheet="' + sheet + '" data-id="">Add</button></div>' + (rows.length ? rows.map(function (r) { return '<div class="task"><div class="t"><b>' + esc(r.Title || r.Name || r.Note || '') + '</b><div class="small muted">' + sub(r) + '</div></div><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="editRow" data-sheet="' + sheet + '" data-id="' + esc(r.Id) + '">Edit</button></div>'; }).join('') : '<p class="muted">None yet.</p>') + '</div>'; }
  var body = '';
  if (tab === 'profile') body = '<div class="card">' + rowForm('Clients', c) + '</div>';
  if (tab === 'logins') body = '<div class="card"><h3>Taxpayer logins</h3><p class="small muted">Each person logs in with their last name and the last 4 of their SSN. Only a salted hash is stored; you will never see the digits again after saving. To change the last 4, enter it again.</p>' + d.logins.map(function (l) { return '<div class="task"><div class="t"><b>' + esc(l.Label) + '</b> \u00b7 ' + esc(l.LastName) + loginExtra(l.Id) + '<div class="small muted">' + (l.isSet ? 'Last 4 set ' + day(l.UpdatedAt) : 'Not set') + (num(l.Fails) ? ' \u00b7 ' + l.Fails + ' wrong tries' : '') + (l.LockedUntil && Date.parse(l.LockedUntil) > Date.now() ? ' \u00b7 <span class="pill bad">paused</span>' : '') + '</div></div><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="editLogin" data-id="' + esc(l.Id) + '">Change last 4</button>' + (num(l.Fails) || l.LockedUntil ? '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="unlockLogin" data-id="' + esc(l.Id) + '">Unlock</button>' : '') + '<button class="btn sm bad" data-act="deleteRow" data-sheet="Logins" data-id="' + esc(l.Id) + '">Remove</button></div>'; }).join('') + '<button class="btn navy" data-act="editLogin" data-id="">Add a login</button></div>';
  if (tab === 'sections') body = list('Sections', d.sections, 'Plan sections', function (r) { return 'Order ' + esc(r.Order) + (r.Visible === 'N' ? ' \u00b7 hidden' : '') + ' \u00b7 ' + esc(String(r.Summary).slice(0, 120)); }) + '<div class="card"><h3>Corrections</h3>' + d.corrections.map(function (x) { return '<div class="c small"><b>' + esc(x.SectionTitle) + '</b> \u00b7 ' + esc(x.Person) + ' ' + statusPill(x.Status) + '<div>' + esc(x.Text) + '</div>' + (x.Reply ? '<div class="reply">' + esc(x.Reply) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  if (tab === 'strategies') body = list('Strategies', d.strategies, 'Strategies', function (r) { return esc(r.Num) + ' \u00b7 ' + esc(r.Grp) + (r.Visible === 'N' ? ' \u00b7 hidden' : ''); });
  if (tab === 'tasks') body = list('Tasks', d.tasks, 'Tasks', function (r) { return esc(r.Owner) + (r.Due ? ' \u00b7 due ' + esc(r.Due) : '') + (r.Done === 'Y' ? ' \u00b7 done' : '') + (r.Proposed === 'Y' ? ' \u00b7 proposed' : ''); });
  if (tab === 'holdings') { var t = netTotals(d.holdings); body = '<div class="grid g3"><div class="stat">Own<b>' + money(t.own) + '</b></div><div class="stat">Owe<b>' + money(t.owe) + '</b></div><div class="stat">Net<b>' + money(t.net) + '</b></div></div><div class="card" style="margin-top:14px">' + d.holdings.map(function (h) { return '<div class="task"><div class="t"><b>' + esc(h.Name) + '</b><div class="small muted">' + esc(h.Category) + ' \u00b7 value ' + (h.Value ? money(h.Value) : '-') + ' \u00b7 owed ' + (h.Owed ? money(h.Owed) : '-') + ' \u00b7 by ' + esc(h.UpdatedBy || '') + '</div></div><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="adminHolding" data-id="' + esc(h.Id) + '">Edit</button></div>'; }).join('') + '<button class="btn navy" data-act="adminHolding" data-id="">Add item</button></div>'; }
  if (tab === 'forms') body = '<div class="card"><h3>Forms and uploads</h3>' + (d.formSubs.length ? d.formSubs.map(function (f) { var data = null; try { data = JSON.parse(f.DataJson || 'null'); } catch (e) {} return '<details><summary>' + esc(f.Title) + ' <span class="pill">' + esc(f.Status) + '</span></summary><div class="in"><div class="small muted">' + esc(f.Person) + ' \u00b7 ' + day(f.CreatedAt) + (f.SignedName ? ' \u00b7 signed by ' + esc(f.SignedName) : '') + '</div>' + (data ? '<table class="t">' + Object.keys(data).map(function (k) { return '<tr><td>' + esc(k) + '</td><td>' + esc(data[k]) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table>' : '') + (f.PdfId ? '<p><a class="btn sm ghost" href="https://drive.google.com/file/d/' + esc(f.PdfId) + '/view" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open file</a></p>' : '') + '<form data-form="formUpdate" data-id="' + esc(f.Id) + '"><div class="row"><select name="status">' + ['submitted', 'preparing', 'signature', 'filed', 'done'].map(function (s) { return '<option' + (s === f.Status ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select><button class="btn sm navy" type="submit">Save</button></div><label>Firm note (not shown to the client)<textarea name="note">' + esc(f.FirmNote || '') + '</textarea></label></form><button class="btn sm bad" data-act="deleteRow" data-sheet="FormSubs" data-id="' + esc(f.Id) + '">Delete record</button></div></details>'; }).join('') : '<p class="muted">No forms yet.</p>') + '</div>';
  if (tab === 'activity') body = '<div class="card"><h3>Goals</h3>' + d.goals.map(function (g) { return '<div class="c"><b>' + esc(g.Title) + '</b> ' + statusPill(g.Status) + (g.ReviewNote ? '<div class="reply">' + esc(g.ReviewNote) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div><div class="card"><h3>Questions</h3>' + d.posts.map(function (p) { return '<div class="c"><b>' + esc(p.Text) + '</b> ' + statusPill(p.ReviewStatus) + (p.ReviewNote ? '<div class="reply">' + esc(p.ReviewNote) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  if (tab === 'referrals') body = '<div class="card"><h3>Referrals from this client</h3>' + (d.referrals.map(leadRow).join('') || '<p class="muted">None.</p>') + '</div>';
  if (tab === 'apps') body = list('Apps', d.apps, 'Linked apps (show in the client\'s Apps tab)', function (r) { return esc(r.Url) + (r.Visible === 'N' ? ' \u00b7 hidden' : ''); });
  if (tab === 'merch') body = list('Merch', d.merch, 'Merch plan', function (r) { return (r.SourceDocId ? 'Auto-merge from Doc ' + esc(r.SourceDocId) : 'No Doc linked') + (r.SyncedAt ? ' \u00b7 merged ' + day(r.SyncedAt) : '') + (r.SyncNote ? ' \u00b7 ' + esc(r.SyncNote) : ''); }) + d.merch.filter(function (m) { return m.SourceDocId; }).map(function (m) { return '<button class="btn sm navy" data-act="merchSync" data-id="' + esc(m.Id) + '">Merge "' + esc(m.Title) + '" now</button> '; }).join('');
  if (tab === 'consents') { var ct = A().consentText; body = '<div class="card"><h3>Current consents</h3><table class="t"><tr><th>Consent</th><th>Status</th><th>When</th><th>By</th></tr>' + Object.keys(ct).map(function (k) { var s = d.consentState[k]; return '<tr><td>' + esc(ct[k].title) + '</td><td>' + (s ? esc(s.status) : '-') + '</td><td>' + (s ? day(s.at) : '') + '</td><td>' + (s ? esc(s.by) : '') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table></div><div class="card"><h3>Record a paper or verbal consent</h3><form data-form="adminConsent"><label>Consent<select name="type">' + Object.keys(ct).map(function (k) { return '<option value="' + k + '">' + esc(ct[k].title) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label><label>Person<input name="person" required></label><label>Signed name (for signed consents)<input name="signedName"></label><label>Detail (phone, recipient, how obtained)<input name="detail"></label><div class="row"><select name="grant"><option value="1">Granted</option><option value="">Revoked</option></select><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Record</button></div></form></div><div class="card"><h3>History</h3><table class="t">' + d.consents.slice().reverse().map(function (x) { return '<tr><td>' + day(x.At) + '</td><td>' + esc(x.Type) + '</td><td>' + esc(x.Status) + '</td><td>' + esc(x.Person) + '</td><td>' + esc(x.Method) + '</td><td>v' + esc(x.Version) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table></div>'; }
  if (tab === 'notes') body = list('Notes', d.notes, 'Firm-only notes (never shown to the client)', function (r) { return day(r.CreatedAt); });
  body = editorV2(tab, d, body);
  return head + body;
}
function leadRow(r) {
  var ok = r.SmsConsent === 'Y', hr = detroitHour(), quiet = hr < 8 || hr >= 21;
  var sms = 'Hi ' + first(r.Name) + ', this is ' + B.preparer + ' with ' + B.name + '. ' + (r.MayName === 'Y' && r.ReferrerLabel ? first(r.ReferrerLabel) + ' said you might like help with taxes. ' : '') + 'Want to set up a quick call? Reply STOP to opt out.';
  return '<details><summary>' + esc(r.Name) + ' <span class="pill ' + ({ signed: 'good', declined: 'bad', contacted: 'gold' }[r.Status] || '') + '">' + esc(r.Status || 'new') + '</span>' + (r.Contacted === 'Y' ? ' <span class="pill good">contacted</span>' : '') + '</summary><div class="in">' +
    '<div class="small">From: ' + esc(r.ReferrerLabel) + (r.ClientId ? ' (' + esc(cname(r.ClientId)) + ')' : '') + ' \u00b7 ' + day(r.CreatedAt) + ' \u00b7 ' + esc(r.Relation || '') + '<br>Email: ' + esc(r.Email || '-') + (r.EmailOptOut === 'Y' ? ' <span class="pill bad">opted out</span>' : '') + ' \u00b7 Phone: ' + esc(r.Phone || '-') + '<br>May name referrer: ' + (r.MayName === 'Y' ? 'yes' : 'no') + ' \u00b7 Welcome email: ' + (r.WelcomeAt ? day(r.WelcomeAt) : 'not sent') + (r.Error ? ' \u00b7 <span class="pill bad">' + esc(r.Error) + '</span>' : '') + '<br>Text consent: ' + (ok ? '<span class="pill good">yes ' + day(r.SmsConsentAt) + '</span> <span class="muted">' + esc(r.SmsConsentText || '') + '</span>' : '<span class="pill warn">none</span>') + '</div><p>' + esc(r.Need || '') + '</p>' +
    '<form data-form="leadUpdate" data-id="' + esc(r.Id) + '"><div class="row"><div><label>Status<select name="status">' + ['new', 'contacted', 'signed', 'declined'].map(function (s) { return '<option' + (s === (r.Status || 'new') ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + s + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label></div><div><label class="check" style="margin-top:34px"><input type="checkbox" name="contacted"' + (r.Contacted === 'Y' ? ' checked' : '') + '> We contacted them</label></div></div><label>Firm notes<textarea name="notes">' + esc(r.Notes || '') + '</textarea></label><button class="btn sm navy" type="submit">Save</button></form>' +
    '<h3>Text draft</h3>' + (ok ? (quiet ? '<div class="err">It is outside 8 a.m. to 9 p.m. in Michigan. Wait to send (47 CFR 64.1200(c)(1)); check the person\'s own time zone too.</div>' : '') + '<textarea readonly>' + esc(sms) + '</textarea><p class="small muted">Send from your business line. Honor STOP right away.</p>' : '<p class="small muted">No texts until they opt in (TCPA). Their welcome email has an opt-in link, or record written consent below.</p>') +
    '<div class="row" style="gap:8px;margin-top:8px">' + (r.Email ? '<button class="btn sm" data-act="leadWelcome" data-id="' + esc(r.Id) + '">' + (r.WelcomeAt ? 'Resend' : 'Send') + ' welcome email</button>' : '') + (ok ? '' : '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="leadSms" data-id="' + esc(r.Id) + '">Record text consent</button>') + '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="leadOptOut" data-id="' + esc(r.Id) + '">Record opt-out</button><button class="btn sm bad" data-act="deleteLead" data-id="' + esc(r.Id) + '">Delete</button></div></div></details>';
}

function afterClient(d, msg) { if (d) { S.bundle = d; renderClient(); if (msg || d.note) toast(msg || d.note); } }

function loadClient(id, tab) { return api('adminClient', { clientId: id }).then(function (d) { S.cur = d; S.ctab = tab || S.ctab || 'profile'; S.aview = 'clients'; renderAdmin(); }, function (e) { toast(e.message); }); }
function findIn(arr, id) { for (var i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) if (arr[i].Id === id) return arr[i]; return null; }

function readFile(input, max) { return new Promise(function (res, rej) { var f = input.files && input.files[0]; if (!f) { rej(new Error('Choose a file first.')); return; } if (f.size > max) { rej(new Error('That file is too large.')); return; } var r = new FileReader(); r.onload = function () { res({ name: f.name, dataUrl: r.result }); }; r.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('Could not read that file.')); }; r.readAsDataURL(f); }); }
