    run(btn, 'saveRow', { sheet: sheet, row: row }, 'adminok').then(function (r) { closeModal(); if (sheet === 'Tips') { setAdmin(r); } else if (sheet === 'Clients') { setAdmin(r.boot); S.cur = r.client; S.aview = 'clients'; } else S.cur = r; renderAdmin(); toast('Saved.'); }, function () {});
  },
  setLast4: function (f, d, btn) { d.id = f.dataset.id; d.clientId = S.cur.client.Id; run(btn, 'setLast4', d, 'adminok').then(function (r) { closeModal(); S.cur = r; renderAdmin(); toast('Login saved. The digits were not stored.'); }, function () {}); },
  formUpdate: function (f, d, btn) { run(btn, 'formUpdate', { id: f.dataset.id, status: d.status, note: d.note }, 'adminok').then(function (r) { S.cur = r; renderAdmin(); toast('Saved.'); return api('adminBoot', {}).then(setAdmin); }, function () {}); },
  adminConsent: function (f, d, btn) { run(btn, 'consentSet', { clientId: S.cur.client.Id, type: d.type, grant: !!d.grant, revoke: !d.grant, person: d.person, signedName: d.signedName, detail: d.detail }, 'adminok').then(function (r) { S.cur = r; renderAdmin(); toast('Recorded.'); }, function () {}); },
  leadUpdate: function (f, d, btn) { run(btn, 'leadUpdate', { id: f.dataset.id, status: d.status, contacted: d.contacted, notes: d.notes }, 'adminok').then(function (r) { setAdmin(r); refreshAfterLead(); toast('Saved.'); }, function () {}); },
  leadSms: function (f, d, btn) { run(btn, 'leadUpdate', { id: f.dataset.id, smsConsent: true, how: d.how }, 'adminok').then(function (r) { closeModal(); setAdmin(r); refreshAfterLead(); toast('Consent recorded.'); }, function () {}); },
  saveSettings: function (f, d, btn) { var key = d.claudeKey; delete d.claudeKey; run(btn, 'saveSettings', { settings: d, claudeKey: key }, 'adminok').then(function (r) { setAdmin(r); renderAdmin(); toast('Saved.'); }, function () {}); },
  changePin: function (f, d, btn) { run(btn, 'changePin', d, 'adminok').then(function () { f.reset(); toast('PIN changed.'); }, function () {}); },
  uploadLogo: function (f, d, btn) { readFile($('#logofile', f), 3000000).then(function (file) { return run(btn, 'uploadLogo', { dataUrl: file.dataUrl }, 'adminok'); }).then(function (r) { if (r) { setAdmin(r); B = r.brand; renderAdmin(); toast('Logo saved.'); } }, function (e) { toast(e.message); }); }
};
document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
  var el = e.target.closest('[data-act]'); if (!el) return;
  var fn = ACT[el.dataset.act]; if (!fn) return;
  if (el.tagName === 'A' && el.getAttribute('href') === '#') e.preventDefault();
  if (el.type !== 'checkbox') e.preventDefault();
  try { fn(el, e); } catch (err) { toast(err.message); }
});
document.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
  var f = e.target.closest('form[data-form]'); if (!f) return;
  e.preventDefault();
  var fn = FORM[f.dataset.form]; if (!fn) return;
  try { fn(f, formData(f), f.querySelector('button[type=submit]')); } catch (err) { toast(err.message); }
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') closeModal(); });

/* =================================================================
   v2.0: people, separate logins, messages, documents, MyTaxOffice,
   plan updates to approve, team logins. (Added; nothing above removed.)
   ================================================================= */
function V2() { var b = C(); return (b && b.v2) || { person: '', people: [], logins: [], messages: [], docs: [], splitAdvice: false, filingStatus: '', portalGuide: '' }; }
function ME() { var a = A(); return (a && a.v2 && a.v2.me) || { owner: true, perms: [], name: '' }; }
function canView(v) { var p = AVIEW_PERM[v]; if (ME().owner) return true; if (!p || p === 'OWNER') return !p; return ME().perms.indexOf(p) > -1; }
function personOf(name) { var l = V2().people; for (var i = 0; i < l.length; i++) if (l[i].Name === name) return l[i]; return null; }
function picHtml(photo, theme) { var fb = (C() && C().client && C().client.theme) || (S.cur && S.cur.client && S.cur.client.Theme) || 'lake'; return photo ? '<img src="' + esc(photo) + '" alt="">' : scene(theme || fb); }
function whoChips() {
  var ppl = V2().people; if (ppl.length < 2) return '';
  return '<div class="chips" role="group" aria-label="Whose plan">' + [{ Name: '', label: 'Household (joint)' }].concat(ppl).map(function (p) { var k = p.Name || ''; return '<button class="' + ((S.who || '') === k ? 'on' : '') + '" data-act="whoSet" data-k="' + esc(k) + '">' + esc(p.label || p.Name) + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
}
function homeV2() {
  var v = V2(), b = C(), out = '';
  var unread = v.messages.filter(function (m) { return m.dir === 'out' && m.status === 'sent'; }).length;
  if (unread) out += '<div class="banner"><span class="pill gold">NEW</span><span>You have ' + unread + ' new message' + (unread > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' from ' + esc(B.signAs || B.name) + '.</span><span class="sp"></span><button class="btn sm navy" data-act="cview" data-k="messages">Read</button></div>';
  if (v.people.length) out += '<h2>' + (v.people.length > 1 ? 'Your household' : 'You') + '</h2><div class="grid g3">' + v.people.map(function (p) { return '<button class="gcard" data-act="whoWork" data-k="' + esc(p.Name) + '"><div class="pic">' + picHtml(p.photo, p.Theme) + '</div><div class="bd"><b>' + esc(p.FullName || p.Name) + '</b><span class="small muted">' + esc(p.Occupation || p.Role || '') + '</span>' + ((p.fields || []).length ? '<div class="small" style="margin-top:6px">' + p.fields.length + ' field' + (p.fields.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' of work · tap to see methods</div>' : '') + '</div></button>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  var goals = b.goals.slice().reverse().slice(0, 4);
  if (goals.length) out += '<div class="card" style="margin-top:16px"><div class="row"><h3 style="flex:1 1 auto">Your goals</h3><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="cview" data-k="goals">All goals</button></div>' + goals.map(function (g) { return '<div class="task"><div class="t"><b>' + esc(g.Title) + '</b><div class="small muted">' + esc(g.Person || '') + ' · ' + statusPill(g.Status) + '</div></div></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  var t = B.taxOffice || {};
  if (t.url) out += '<div class="banner" style="margin-top:16px"><span class="pill">MyTaxOffice</span><span class="small">Signed returns, signature requests and copies of your tax documents are in your MyTaxOffice portal.</span><span class="sp"></span><button class="btn sm navy" data-act="cview" data-k="docs">Open Documents</button></div>';
  return out;
}

/* ---------- forms helpers ---------- */
function fillPerson(f, force) {
  var sel = $('[data-person]', f); if (!sel) return;
  var p = personOf(sel.value); if (!p) return;
  var map = { personName: p.FullName || p.Name, personAddress: p.Address || '', personPhone: p.Phone || '' };
  $$('[data-pf]', f).forEach(function (el) { if (force || !el.value) el.value = map[el.dataset.pf] || el.value; });
}
function dupModal(b, go) {
  var m = modal('<h2>Already sent?</h2><p>' + esc(b.message) + '</p><div class="card"><table class="t"><tr><th>Sent</th><th>For</th><th>Detail</th><th>Status</th></tr>' + (b.prev || []).map(function (x) { return '<tr><td>' + esc(x.at) + '</td><td>' + esc(x.person) + '</td><td>' + esc(x.employer || '-') + '</td><td>' + esc(x.status) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table></div>' +
    '<div class="row"><button class="btn navy" id="dupReplace">Replace the earlier one</button><button class="btn" id="dupKeep">Keep both (this is a separate one)</button><button class="btn ghost" data-act="closeModal">Cancel</button></div><p class="small muted">Two jobs = two W-4s, one for each employer. That is normal. Replace only if you are fixing one you already sent.</p>');
  $('#dupReplace', m).onclick = function (e) { go({ replaceId: b.prev[0].id }, e.target); };
  $('#dupKeep', m).onclick = function (e) { go({ confirmDup: true }, e.target); };
}
function showFile(r) {
  try {
    var bin = atob(r.dataUrl.split(',')[1]), arr = new Uint8Array(bin.length); for (var i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) arr[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
    var url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([arr], { type: r.dataUrl.slice(5, r.dataUrl.indexOf(';')) }));
    var m = modal('<h2>Your copy</h2><p class="small muted">' + esc(r.name) + '</p><div class="row"><a class="btn navy" href="' + url + '" download="' + esc(r.name) + '">Save to my device</a><a class="btn ghost" href="' + url + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open in a new tab</a></div><iframe title="Document" src="' + url + '" style="width:100%;height:60vh;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;background:#fff;margin-top:10px"></iframe>', true);
    return m;
  } catch (e) { toast('Could not open the file on this device.'); }
}

/* ---------- client views ---------- */
/* v2.1: apps grouped by line of work, command centers first, search */
var APP_CAT_ORDER = ['All businesses (command centers)', 'Crispus Mundo Tax', 'Crisp Cares Heating & Cooling', 'CMCC Training Institute', 'Crisp Cares Environmental Services', 'Crisp Cares Medical', 'Crisp Clean merchandising & LMIRA', 'Nonprofit & community (501(c)(3))', 'Technology, legal tech & research', 'Personal & family', 'Needs a category'];
function normK(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''); }
function appBtnLabel(a) { return /script\.google\.com\/(home\/projects|d\/)/.test(String(a.Url)) ? 'Open in Apps Script' : (a.Kind === 'website' ? 'Visit site' : 'Open'); }
function appSort(x, y) { var cx = x.Kind === 'command center' ? 0 : 1, cy = y.Kind === 'command center' ? 0 : 1; return cx - cy || (Number(x.Order || 0) - Number(y.Order || 0)) || String(x.Title).localeCompare(String(y.Title)); }
function appCardHtml(a) { return '<div class="card appc" data-q="' + esc(String((a.Title || '') + ' ' + (a.Description || '') + ' ' + (a.Category || '') + ' ' + (a.Kind || '')).toLowerCase()) + '"><h3>' + esc(a.Title) + '</h3>' + (a.Kind === 'command center' ? '<span class="pill gold">Command center</span> ' : (a.Kind ? '<span class="pill">' + esc(a.Kind) + '</span> ' : '')) + (a.Person ? '<span class="pill">' + esc(a.Person) + '</span>' : '') + '<p>' + esc(a.Description) + '</p><a class="btn navy" href="' + esc(a.Url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + appBtnLabel(a) + '</a></div>'; }
function appFilter(q) {
  q = String(q || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.appc'), function (el) { el.style.display = !q || el.getAttribute('data-q').indexOf(q) > -1 ? '' : 'none'; });
  Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll('.appgrp'), function (g) { var any = Array.prototype.some.call(g.querySelectorAll('.appc'), function (el) { return el.style.display !== 'none'; }); g.style.display = any ? '' : 'none'; });
}
function appGroups(list) {
  var by = {}, keys = [];
  list.forEach(function (a) { var k = a.Category || ''; if (!by[k]) { by[k] = []; keys.push(k); } by[k].push(a); });
  keys.sort(function (x, y) { var ix = APP_CAT_ORDER.indexOf(x), iy = APP_CAT_ORDER.indexOf(y); ix = ix < 0 ? (x ? 50 : 99) : ix; iy = iy < 0 ? (y ? 50 : 99) : iy; return ix - iy || x.localeCompare(y); });
  return keys.map(function (k) { return { cat: k, apps: by[k].sort(appSort) }; });
}
CV.apps = function () {
  var who = S.who || '', list = C().apps.filter(function (a) { return !who || !a.Person || a.Person === who; });
  var groups = appGroups(list), many = list.length > 8;
  var head = '<h1>Your apps &amp; documents</h1><p class="muted">' + (groups.length > 1 ? 'Sorted by line of work. Command centers come first in each group.' : 'Tools and documents we built or set up for you.') + '</p>' + whoChips();
  if (many) head += '<div class="card"><input type="search" placeholder="Search your ' + list.length + ' apps and documents" oninput="appFilter(this.value)" aria-label="Search apps"></div>';
  if (groups.length > 1) head += '<div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-bottom:10px">' + groups.map(function (g, i) { return '<button class="pill" type="button" onclick="var e=document.getElementById(\'appgrp-' + i + '\');if(e)e.scrollIntoView({behavior:\'smooth\'})">' + esc(g.cat || 'Other') + ' (' + g.apps.length + ')</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  var body = groups.length > 1 ? groups.map(function (g, i) { return '<section class="appgrp" id="appgrp-' + i + '"><h2>' + esc(g.cat || 'Other') + '</h2><div class="grid g2">' + g.apps.map(appCardHtml).join('') + '</div></section>'; }).join('') : '<div class="grid g2">' + list.slice().sort(appSort).map(appCardHtml).join('') + '</div>';
  return head + body + '<p class="small muted">If a document asks you to request access, press Request access or tell us in Messages and we will share it with your email.</p>';
};
function appsForField(f) {
  var keys = [normK(f.category), normK(f.business), normK(f.title)].filter(String);
  return C().apps.filter(function (a) { return a.Category && keys.indexOf(normK(a.Category)) > -1; }).sort(appSort);
}
function appLinksHtml(apps, title) { return apps.length ? '<h3 style="font-size:1rem;margin-top:10px">' + esc(title) + '</h3><div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px">' + apps.map(function (a) { return '<a class="btn sm ' + (a.Kind === 'command center' ? 'navy' : 'ghost') + '" style="max-width:100%;white-space:normal;text-align:left;height:auto" href="' + esc(a.Url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + esc(a.Title) + '</a>'; }).join('') + '</div>' : ''; }

  var v = V2(), who = S.who || '';

  return '<h1>' + (v.people.length > 1 ? 'Our work' : 'My work') + '</h1><p class="muted">Each line of work, with the tax methods that fit it. Tell us if anything changed.</p>' + whoChips() +
    (function () { var all = C().apps.filter(function (a) { return normK(a.Category) === normK(APP_CAT_ORDER[0]); }).sort(appSort); return all.length ? '<div class="card"><h3>Across all businesses</h3>' + appLinksHtml(all, 'Command centers') + '</div>' : ''; })() +
    ppl.map(function (p) {

        (p.Occupation ? '<p>' + esc(p.Occupation) + '</p>' : '') + '<div class="small">' + (p.WorkType ? '<b>Work type:</b> ' + esc(p.WorkType) + '<br>' : '') + (p.Employers ? '<b>Employers:</b> ' + esc(p.Employers) + '<br>' : '') + '<b>Email for notices:</b> ' + esc(p.Email || 'not on file') + '</div>' +
        (S.preview ? '' : '<button class="btn sm ghost" style="margin-top:8px" data-act="editMe" data-id="' + esc(p.Id) + '">Update ' + esc(p.Name) + '\'s info</button>') + '</div></div>' +
        ((p.fields || []).length ? '<div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:14px">' + p.fields.map(function (f) { return '<div class="fcard"><div class="pic">' + picHtml(f.photo, f.theme) + '</div><div class="bd"><div class="cinzel small" style="color:var(--gold2)">' + esc(String(f.business || '').toUpperCase()) + '</div><b style="font-family:\'Cormorant Garamond\',Georgia,serif;font-size:1.25rem">' + esc(f.title) + '</b>' + (f.about ? '<p class="small">' + esc(f.about) + '</p>' : '') + ((f.methods || []).length ?     }).join('') + '<p class="small muted">Methods are general rules for this kind of work. Your plan says what applies to you.</p>';
};

};

Use Send us a document, or Forms.</p>') + '<p class="small muted">Form copies (W-4 answer sheets and more) are in Forms &gt; What you sent us.</p></div>';

/* ---------- admin: queue cards ---------- */
function hasProposal(corrId) { var l = (A().queue.proposals || []); for (var i = 0; i < l.length; i++) if (l[i].SourceId === corrId) return l[i].Id; return ''; }
function propBody(p) {
  var a = p.after || {}, bf = p.before || {};
  var ta = function (n, label, v, rows) { return '<label>' + label + '<textarea name="a_' + n + '" rows="' + (rows || 4) + '">' + esc(v || '') + '</textarea></label>'; };
  var inp = function (n, label, v) { return '<label>' + label + '<input name="a_' + n + '" value="' + esc(v || '') + '"></label>'; };
  if (p.Target === 'Sections' && p.Action === 'update') return '<details><summary>Current text in the plan</summary><div class="in"><div class="diff">' + esc(bf.Summary || '') + '\n\n' + esc(bf.Points || '') + '</div></div></details>' + ta('Summary', 'New summary', a.Summary, 3) + ta('Points', 'New points (one per line)', a.Points, 8);
  if (p.Target === 'Sections' && p.Action === 'add') return inp('Title', 'Section title', a.Title) + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('Person', 'Person (blank = household)', a.Person) + '</div><div>' + inp('Order', 'Order', a.Order) + '</div></div>' + ta('Summary', 'Summary', a.Summary, 3) + ta('Points', 'Points (one per line)', a.Points, 7) + inp('Codes', 'Law cited', a.Codes);
  if (p.Target === 'Holdings') return '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('Name', 'Item', a.Name) + '</div><div>' + inp('Category', 'Type', a.Category) + '</div></div><div class="row"><div>' + inp('Value', 'Value ($)', a.Value) + '</div><div>' + inp('Owed', 'Owed ($)', a.Owed) + '</div></div>' + inp('Note', 'Note', a.Note);
  if (p.Target === 'Tasks') return '<p class="small">' + (a.taskIds || []).length + ' open task(s) will be checked off.</p>';
  return '';
}
function proposalCard(p, back) {
  var a = p.after || {};
  return '<div class="card" id="prop-' + esc(p.Id) + '"><div class="small muted">' + esc(p.ClientName || cname(p.ClientId)) + ' · from ' + esc(p.Source) + ' · ' + esc(p.By || '') + ' · ' + day(p.CreatedAt) + '</div><h3>' + esc(p.Title) + '</h3>' + (p.Why ? '<div class="banner small" style="display:block">' + esc(p.Why) + '</div>' : '') +
    '<form data-form="proposalApply" data-id="' + esc(p.Id) + '" data-back="' + (back || '') + '">' + propBody(p) +
    ((a.tasks || []).length ? '<p class="small"><b>Tasks that will be added:</b> ' + a.tasks.map(function (t) { return esc(t.title) + ' (' + esc(t.owner || 'Cyars') + ')'; }).join('; ') + '</p>' : '') +
    (p.Source === 'correction' || p.Source === 'goal' || p.Reply ? '<label>Reply to the client (edit if needed)<textarea name="reply" rows="4">' + esc(p.Reply || '') + '</textarea></label>' : '') +
    '<div class="row"><button class="btn sm navy" type="submit">Apply' + (p.Reply || p.Source === 'correction' ? ' and send reply' : '') + '</button><button class="btn sm ghost" type="button" data-act="proposalReject" data-id="' + esc(p.Id) + '" data-back="' + (back || '') + '">Reject</button>' + (back ? '' : '<button class="btn sm ghost" type="button" data-act="openClient" data-id="' + esc(p.ClientId) + '" data-tab="sections">Open the plan</button>') + '</div></form></div>';
}
function msgCard(m, back) {
  return '<div class="card"><div class="small muted">' + esc(cname(m.ClientId)) + ' · ' + esc(m.Person || m.By) + ' · ' + esc(String(m.CreatedAt).slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' ')) + '</div><p><b>' + esc(m.Text) + '</b></p>' +
    '<form data-form="msgReply" data-id="' + esc(m.Id) + '" data-back="' + (back || '') + '"><label>Reply (shown in their portal; they get an email notice)' + (m.AiDraft ? ' <span class="pill gold">AI draft, edit freely</span>' : '') + '<textarea name="text" rows="5">' + esc(m.AiDraft || '') + '</textarea></label><div class="row"><button class="btn sm navy" type="submit">Send reply</button><button class="btn sm" type="button" data-act="msgDraft" data-id="' + esc(m.Id) + '">' + (m.AiDraft ? 'Redraft with AI' : 'Draft with AI') + '</button><button class="btn sm ghost" type="button" data-act="msgClose" data-id="' + esc(m.Id) + '">Close without reply</button></div></form></div>';
}

function loginExtra(id) { var l = (S.cur && S.cur.v2 && S.cur.v2.logins) || []; for (var i = 0; i < l.length; i++) if (l[i].Id === id) return (l[i].Person ? ' · <span class="pill">' + esc(l[i].Person) + '</span>' : ' · <span class="pill">household</span>') + (l[i].Email ? ' <span class="small muted">' + esc(l[i].Email) + '</span>' : ''); return ''; }
function loginPersonFields(id) {
  var cur = {}; ((S.cur && S.cur.v2 && S.cur.v2.logins) || []).forEach(function (l) { if (l.Id === id) cur = l; });
  var ppl = (S.cur && S.cur.v2 && S.cur.v2.people) || [];
  return '<label>Whose login<select name="person"><option value="">Shared household login</option>' + ppl.map(function (p) { return '<option' + (p.Name === cur.Person ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + esc(p.Name) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label><label>Email for this login (activity emails)<input name="email" type="email" value="' + esc(cur.Email || '') + '"></label>';
}
function fieldsToText(arr) { return (arr || []).map(function (f) { return '## ' + (f.title || '') + ' | ' + (f.business || '') + ' | ' + (f.theme || '') + '\n' + (f.about || '') + '\n' + (f.methods || []).map(function (m) { return '- ' + m; }).join('\n'); }).join('\n\n'); }
function textToFields(txt, old) {
  var out = [], cur = null;
  String(txt || '').split('\n').forEach(function (line) {
    var l = line.trim();
    if (/^##/.test(l)) { var parts = l.replace(/^##\s*/, '').split('|').map(function (x) { return x.trim(); }); cur = { title: parts[0] || 'Work', business: parts[1] || '', theme: parts[2] || 'lake', about: '', methods: [] }; out.push(cur); }
    else if (cur && /^-\s*/.test(l)) cur.methods.push(l.replace(/^-\s*/, ''));
    else if (cur && l) cur.about = (cur.about ? cur.about + ' ' : '') + l;
  });
  out.forEach(function (f, i) { var o = (old || []).filter(function (x) { return x.title === f.title; })[0] || (old || [])[i]; if (o && o.photoId) f.photoId = o.photoId; });
  return out;
}

  return '<h2>' + (p.Id ? 'Edit ' + esc(p.Name) : 'Add a person') + '</h2><form data-form="adminPerson" data-id="' + esc(p.Id || '') + '">' +
    '<div class="row"><div>' + f('Name', 'First name (used for owners and filters)') + '</div><div>' + f('FullName', 'Full legal name (fills forms)') + '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="row"><div>' + f('Role', 'Role (Primary, Spouse / partner, Dependent, Owner)') + '</div><div>' + f('Order', 'Order') + '</div></div>' +
    '<div class="row"><div>' + f('Email', 'Email (notices, activity email)') + '</div><div>' + f('Phone', 'Phone') + '</div></div>' + f('Address', 'Home address (fills forms)', true) +
    f('Occupation', 'Occupation') + f('Employers', 'Employers (separate with ;)') + '<div class="row"><div>' + f('WorkType', 'Work type (W-2, 1099, self-employed...)') + '</div><div>' + f('Theme', 'Picture theme') + '</div></div><div class="help">' + esc(HINT.Theme) + '</div>' + f('Tags', 'Tags (pick tips)') +
    '<label>Fields of work, pictures and methods</label><textarea name="FieldsText" rows="12" placeholder="## Food truck (startup) | Not open yet | food&#10;One line about it&#10;- method one&#10;- method two">' + esc(fieldsToText(p.fields)) + '</textarea><div class="help">One block per field: a line starting with ## then title | business | picture theme, an about line, then one method per line starting with -. Upload real photos after saving.</div>' +
    f('Notes', 'Firm notes about this person', true) + '<div class="row" style="margin-top:12px"><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Save</button>' + (p.Id ? '<button class="btn bad" type="button" data-act="deleteRow" data-sheet="People" data-id="' + esc(p.Id) + '">Delete</button>' : '') + '</div></form>';
}

New apps are added and sorted by line of work; your own edits here are kept.' + ((A().settings || {}).REGISTRY_LAST_SYNC ? ' Last sync: ' + esc(A().settings.REGISTRY_LAST_SYNC) + '.' : '') + '</p><button class="btn sm navy" data-act="registrySync">Sync from Command Center now</button></div>' : '') + body + '<div class="card"><h3>Share documents with the client</h3><p class="small muted">Gives the people on this file view-only access to a Drive document or folder (uses the emails on the People tab).</p>' + d.apps.map(function (a) { return '<div class="task"><div class="t"><b>' + esc(a.Title) + '</b>' + (a.Visible === 'N' ? ' <span class="pill">hidden from client</span>' : '') + (a.Person ? ' <span class="pill">' + esc(a.Person) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' + (/google\.com/.test(a.Url) && !/script\.google/.test(a.Url) ? '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="shareApp" data-id="' + esc(a.Id) + '">Share with client</button>' : '') + '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="copyAppPick" data-id="' + esc(a.Id) + '" data-t="' + esc(a.Title) + '">Copy to another client</button></div>'; }).join('') + '</div>';
  if (tab === 'sections') return '<div class="row" style="margin-bottom:10px">' + aiBtn + '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="ctab" data-k="updates">Plan updates waiting: ' + v.proposals.filter(function (p) { return p.Status === 'pending'; }).length + '</button></div>' + body;
  return body;
}
function howUpdatesCard() {
  return '<div class="card"><h3>How to update a client\'s plan (no code changes)</h3><ol class="steps small"><li>Client sends a correction, goal, message or document: it lands in Queue. If AI is on for that client, a drafted plan change and a drafted reply are waiting under Plan updates to approve.</li><li>Edit the draft if needed and press Apply. The plan, tasks and the client\'s reply update at once, and the client gets an email notice.</li><li>You have a new document? Clients &gt; (client) &gt; Documents &gt; Upload, tick Read with AI. The drafted updates appear in Plan updates.</li><li>Want to change something yourself? Clients &gt; (client) &gt; Plan &gt; Edit. Saves go live right away.</li><li>Only code changes (new features) need Save + Deploy &gt; Manage deployments &gt; New version.</li></ol></div>';
}

function pinModal(r) { if (r && r.newPin) modal('<h2>PIN for ' + esc(r.newPinFor) + '</h2><p style="font-size:2rem" class="cinzel"><b>' + esc(r.newPin) + '</b></p><p>Give this PIN to them privately (not by email). function resizeImage(input, max) {
  return new Promise(function (res, rej) {
    var f = input.files && input.files[0]; if (!f) { rej(new Error('Choose a picture first.')); return; }
    var r = new FileReader(); r.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('Could not read that picture.')); };
    r.onload = function () { var img = new Image(); img.onerror = function () { rej(new Error('That file is not a picture this browser can read.')); }; img.onload = function () { var k = Math.min(1, max / Math.max(img.width, img.height)); var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = Math.round(img.width * k); c.height = Math.round(img.height * k); c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0, c.width, c.height); res(c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.82)); }; img.src = r.result; };
    r.readAsDataURL(f);
  });
}

Drive files still need Share with client on their file.</p><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Copy</button></form>'); },
  registrySync: function (el) { run(el, 'registrySync', { clientId: S.cur && S.cur.client ? S.cur.client.Id : '' }, 'adminok').then(afterAdmin, function () {}); },
  formPdf: function (el) { run(el, 'formPdf', { id: el.dataset.id }, 'adminok').then(function (r) { if (r) showFile(r); }, function () {}); },
  editMe: function (el) { var p = V2().people.filter(function (x) { return x.Id === el.dataset.id; })[0] || {}; modal('<h2>Update ' + esc(p.Name) + '\'s info</h2><form data-form="clientPerson" data-id="' + esc(p.Id) + '"><label>Full legal name<input name="fullName" value="' + esc(p.FullName || '') + '"></label><label>Email for notices<input name="email" type="email" value="' + esc(p.Email || '') + '"></label><label>Phone<input name="phone" value="' + esc(p.Phone || '') + '"></label><label>Home address<textarea name="address">' + esc(p.Address || '') + '</textarea></label><label>Occupation<input name="occupation" value="' + esc(p.Occupation || '') + '"></label><label>Employers<input name="employers" value="' + esc(p.Employers || '') + '"></label><p class="small muted">We get a note when you change this, and your forms fill in from it.</p><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Save</button></form>'); },
  taxReq: function (el) { var k = el.dataset.k; modal('<h2>' + esc(el.textContent) + '</h2><form data-form="taxReq" data-k="' + esc(k) + '"><label>Anything we should know (optional)<textarea name="note" placeholder="For example: the reset email never came"></textarea></label><p class="small muted">Do not type passwords or Social Security numbers.</p><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Send request</button></form>'); },

function canDo(perm) { return ME().owner || ME().perms.indexOf(perm) > -1; }
(function () { var has = CTABS.some(function (t) { return t[0] === 'file'; }); if (!has) CTABS.splice(1, 0, ['file', 'Client file & index']); })();
var qCountV23 = qCount;
qCount = function () { var n = qCountV23(); (A().queue.groups || []).forEach(function (g) { n += g.docs ? 1 : 0; }); return n; };
var editorV23 = editorV2;
editorV2 = function (tab, d, body) { if (tab === 'file') return fileTabV24(d); return editorV23(tab, d, body); };
var queueV23 = AV.queue;
AV.queue = function () {
  var gs = A().queue.groups || [];
  var html = queueV23();
  var sec = '<h2>Document groups to approve <span class="pill">' + gs.length + '</span></h2>' + (gs.length ? gs.map(function (g) { return groupCardV24(g, false); }).join('') : '<p class="muted">Nothing waiting.</p>');
  return html.replace('<h1>Queue</h1>', '<h1>Queue</h1>' + sec);
};
function audPill(a) { return a === 'client' ? '<span class="pill gold">client copy</span>' : (a === 'update' ? '<span class="pill">plan update</span>' : '<span class="pill">firm only</span>'); }
function groupCardV24(g, inClient) {
  var can = canDo('apply');
  return '<div class="card"><div class="small muted">' + (inClient ? '' : esc(g.ClientName) + ' · ') + g.pending + ' waiting · ' + day(g.at) + '</div><h3>' + esc(g.Grp || g.grp) + '</h3>' +
    (g.items ? '<ul class="small">' + g.items.map(function (i) { return '<li>' + esc(i.Title) + ' ' + audPill(i.Audience) + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul>' : '') +
    (can ? '<form data-form="groupDecide" data-c="' + esc(g.ClientId || (S.cur && S.cur.client.Id)) + '" data-g="' + esc(g.Grp || g.grp) + '"><label>Note for the file (optional)<input name="note"></label><div class="row"><button class="btn sm navy" type="submit" data-k="approve">Approve group</button><button class="btn sm ghost" type="button" data-act="groupReject" data-c="' + esc(g.ClientId || (S.cur && S.cur.client.Id)) + '" data-g="' + esc(g.Grp || g.grp) + '">Reject group</button>' + (inClient ? '' : '<button class="btn sm ghost" type="button" data-act="openClient" data-id="' + esc(g.ClientId) + '" data-tab="file">Open client file</button>') + '</div></form>' : '<p class="small muted">Your login can view this group but not approve it.</p>') + '</div>';
}
function fileTabV24(d) {
  var v = d.v24; if (!v) return '<p class="muted">Refresh the page to load the client file.</p>';
  var cid = d.client.Id, can = canDo('apply');
  var links = '<div class="row" style="gap:8px">' +
    (v.taxFolderUrl ? '<a class="btn sm navy" href="' + esc(v.taxFolderUrl) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">TAX CLIENTS folder</a>' : '<span class="small muted">No TAX CLIENTS folder set (Profile: TaxFolderId).</span>') +
    (v.indexDocUrl ? '<a class="btn sm" href="' + esc(v.indexDocUrl) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">00 INDEX</a>' : '') +
    (v.logSheetUrl ? '<a class="btn sm" href="' + esc(v.logSheetUrl) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">00 ACTION INDEX</a>' : '') +
    (d.folderUrl ? '<a class="btn sm ghost" href="' + esc(d.folderUrl) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Portal uploads folder</a>' : '') +
    '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="logMirror" data-id="' + esc(cid) + '">Update the action index now' + (v.logUnsynced ? ' (' + v.logUnsynced + ' new)' : '') + '</button></div>';
  var waiting = v.groups.filter(function (g) { return g.pending; });
  var byGrp = {}; v.files.forEach(function (f) { (byGrp[f.Grp || 'Other'] = byGrp[f.Grp || 'Other'] || []).push(f); });
  var groupsHtml = Object.keys(byGrp).map(function (k) {
    return '<details' + (byGrp[k].some(function (f) { return f.Status === 'pending'; }) ? ' open' : '') + '><summary><b>' + esc(k) + '</b> <span class="small muted">' + byGrp[k].length + ' item(s)</span></summary><div class="in">' + byGrp[k].map(function (f) {
      return '<div class="task"><div class="t"><b>' + esc(f.Title) + '</b> ' + audPill(f.Audience) + ' ' + statusPill(f.Status) + '<div class="small muted">' + esc(f.Kind) + (f.Person ? ' · ' + esc(f.Person) : '') + ' · added by ' + esc(f.AddedBy) + ' ' + day(f.CreatedAt) + (f.DecidedBy ? ' · ' + esc(f.Status) + ' by ' + esc(f.DecidedBy) + ' ' + day(f.DecidedAt) : '') + '</div>' + (f.Note ? '<div class="small">' + esc(f.Note) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>' +
        (f.Open ? '<a class="btn sm ghost" href="' + esc(f.Open) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open</a>' : '') +
        (f.Status === 'pending' && can ? '<button class="btn sm navy" data-act="fileDecide" data-id="' + esc(f.Id) + '" data-k="approve">Approve</button><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="fileDecide" data-id="' + esc(f.Id) + '" data-k="reject">Reject</button>' : '') + '</div>';
    }).join('') + '</div></details>';
  }).join('');
  var addForm = canDo('docs') ? '<details><summary>Add a document link to this client\'s file</summary><div class="in"><form data-form="fileSave"><label>Title<input name="title" required></label><label>Link (Google Doc, Sheet, Drive file or folder)<input name="url" required placeholder="https://"></label><div class="row"><div><label>Group<input name="grp" placeholder="for example 2026-10-20 Documents"></label></div><div><label>Who sees it<select name="audience"><option value="firm">Firm only</option><option value="client">Client copy (shows in their Apps tab after approval)</option></select></label></div></div><div class="row"><div><label>Kind<select name="kind"><option>document</option><option>sheet</option><option>folder</option><option>index</option></select></label></div><div><label>Person (blank = household)<input name="person"></label></div></div><label>Note<input name="note"></label><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Add (waits for approval)</button></form></div></details>' : '';
  var log = '<div class="card"><h3>Index of actions (latest 150)</h3><p class="small muted">Every update and every message or email, from the portal, the team and Gmail (when turned on). Copied every 15 minutes to the 00 ACTION INDEX sheet in this client\'s Drive folder.</p><div class="scroll"><table class="t"><tr><th>When</th><th>Group</th><th>Source</th><th>Who</th><th>What</th></tr>' +
    v.log.map(function (r) { return '<tr><td>' + esc(String(r.At).replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 16)) + '</td><td>' + esc(r.Grp || '') + '</td><td>' + esc(r.Source) + '</td><td>' + esc(r.Actor) + '</td><td>' + esc(r.Action) + (r.Detail ? '<details><summary class="small">details</summary><div class="small" style="white-space:pre-wrap">' + esc(r.Detail) + '</div></details>' : '') + (r.Link ? ' <a class="small" href="' + esc(r.Link) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">open</a>' : '') + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table></div></div>';
  return '<div class="card"><h3>Client file</h3>' + links + '</div>' +
    (waiting.length ? '<h2>Waiting for approval</h2>' + waiting.map(function (g) { var gg = { Grp: g.grp, ClientId: cid, pending: g.pending, at: g.at, items: v.files.filter(function (f) { return f.Grp === g.grp && f.Status === 'pending'; }).map(function (f) { return { Title: f.Title, Audience: f.Audience }; }).concat((d.v2.proposals || []).filter(function (p) { return p.Grp === g.grp && p.Status === 'pending'; }).map(function (p) { return { Title: p.Title, Audience: 'update' }; })) }; return groupCardV24(gg, true); }).join('') : '') +
    '<div class="card"><h3>Documents by group</h3>' + (groupsHtml || '<p class="muted">No documents linked yet.</p>') + addForm + '</div>' + log +
    '<div class="card"><h3>How approvals work</h3><ol class="steps small"><li>New documents and drafted updates arrive in a group (for example one meeting) and wait here and in the Queue.</li><li>You, or a team login with "Apply or reject plan updates", press Approve group (or approve one item). Each decision records who made it.</li><li>An approved client copy appears in the client\'s Apps tab. Firm-only items stay in this tab. Drafted tasks are added to Tasks.</li><li>Everything is written to the index of actions and copied to the client\'s 00 ACTION INDEX in Drive.</li></ol></div>';
}

  logMirror: function (el) { run(el, 'logMirror', { clientId: el.dataset.id }, 'adminok').then(afterAdmin, function () {}); }
};
function confirmInline(el) { if (el.dataset.sure) return true; el.dataset.sure = '1'; el.textContent = 'Press again to reject the group'; setTimeout(function () { delete el.dataset.sure; el.textContent = 'Reject group'; }, 4000); return false; }

(function start() {
  renderPublic();
  var t = store('cc_t');
  if (t) { S.token = t; api('resume', {}).then(function (r) { if (r.role === 'client') { S.role = 'client'; S.bundle = r.bundle; renderClient(); } else if (r.role === 'admin') { S.role = 'admin'; S.admin = r.admin; renderAdmin(); } else { S.token = ''; store('cc_t', null); } }, function () {}); }
})();

<script>
/* Planning tools link (added 2026-10-20; nothing above changed). Shows only for clients switched on in Planning Tools. */
(function () {
  try {
    CV.tools = function () {
      return '<div class="card"><h3>Planning tools</h3><p>Your year-end checklist, the documents we still need, an audit-risk check and your plan in plain words.</p>' +
        '<button class="btn navy" data-act="planTools">Open planning tools</button><p class="small muted" id="ptLink" role="status"></p></div>';
    };
    ACT.planTools = function (btn) {
      run(btn, 'planToolsLink', {}).then(function (d) {
        var el = document.getElementById('ptLink');
        if (el && d && /^https:\/\//.test(d.url)) el.innerHTML = '<a class="btn gold" href="' + esc(d.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Tap to open planning tools</a> <span>This link works for 30 minutes.</span>';
      }, function () {});
    };
    var _rc = renderClient;
    renderClient = function () {
      var want = !!(S.bundle && S.bundle.planTools);
      var at = -1;
      CVIEWS.forEach(function (v, i) { if (v[0] === 'tools') at = i; });
      if (want && at === -1) {
        var pos = -1; CVIEWS.forEach(function (v, i) { if (v[0] === 'docs') pos = i; });
        CVIEWS.splice(pos === -1 ? CVIEWS.length : pos + 1, 0, ['tools', 'Planning tools']);
      }
      if (!want && at !== -1) { CVIEWS.splice(at, 1); if (S.view === 'tools') S.view = 'home'; }
      return _rc.apply(this, arguments);
    };
  } catch (e) {}
})();
</script>
<script>
/* v2.5 Representatives & details to confirm (added 2026-10-20; nothing above changed). */
(function () {
  try {
    function V25() { var b = C(); return (b && b.v25) || { facts: [], links: [], acting: null, estate: false, relationships: [] }; }
    function factPill(st) { return st === 'confirmed' ? '<span class="pill good">Confirmed</span>' : (st === 'changed' ? '<span class="pill gold">Sent to our team</span>' : '<span class="pill">Please review</span>'); }
    function factRow(f) {
      var ro = !!S.preview, val = f.value ? esc(f.value) : '<span class="muted">Not on file yet</span>';
      var form = '<form data-form="factSend" data-id="' + esc(f.id) + '" style="' + (!f.value && f.status === 'open' ? '' : 'display:none;') + 'margin-top:8px"><label>' + (f.value ? 'What should it say?' : 'Please add it here') + '<textarea name="text" rows="2" maxlength="1000"></textarea></label><button class="btn sm navy" type="submit">Send</button></form>';
      var act = '';
      if (!ro && f.status === 'open' && f.value) act = '<div class="row" style="gap:8px;margin-top:8px"><button class="btn sm navy" data-act="factYes" data-id="' + esc(f.id) + '">Yes, that\'s right</button><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="factNo" data-id="' + esc(f.id) + '">No, it needs a change</button></div>';
      if (!ro && f.status === 'confirmed') act = '<button class="btn sm ghost" style="margin-top:8px" data-act="factNo" data-id="' + esc(f.id) + '">Something changed?</button>';
      var sent = f.status === 'changed' ? '<div class="small" style="margin-top:6px">You sent: "' + esc(f.answer) + '". We will update your file once we review it.</div>' : '';
      return '<div class="task" style="display:block"><div class="row" style="gap:8px;align-items:flex-start"><div style="flex:1 1 auto"><div class="small muted">' + esc(f.label) + '</div><b>' + val + '</b>' + (f.help ? '<div class="small muted">' + esc(f.help) + '</div>' : '') + '</div>' + factPill(f.status) + '</div>' + sent + act + (ro || f.status === 'changed' ? '' : form) + '</div>';
    }
    function factsCard() {
      var fs = V25().facts; if (!fs.length) return '';
      var open = fs.filter(function (f) { return f.status === 'open'; }).length;
      return '<div class="card" id="factsCard"><h2>Please confirm the details we have on file</h2><p class="small">Tap <b>Yes</b> if a detail is right, or <b>No</b> to tell us what it should say. Nothing on your file changes until our team reviews it with you.' + (open ? ' <b>' + open + ' to review.</b>' : ' Thank you, everything has been reviewed.') + '</p>' + fs.map(factRow).join('') + '</div>';
    }
    function linksCard(full) {
      var ls = V25().links; if (!ls.length && !full) return '';
      var rows = ls.map(function (l) {
        var st = l.status === 'active' ? '<button class="btn sm navy" data-act="repOpen" data-id="' + esc(l.id) + '" data-n="' + esc(l.name) + '">Open</button>' : '<span class="pill">Waiting on our team</span>';
        return '<div class="task"><div class="t"><b>' + esc(l.name) + '</b><div class="small muted">You are the ' + esc(l.relationship) + (l.person ? ' (' + esc(l.person) + ')' : '') + '</div></div>' + (S.preview ? '' : st) + '</div>';
      }).join('');
      return '<div class="card"><h3>Accounts you manage</h3>' + (rows || '<p class="muted small">None linked yet.</p>') + '<p class="small muted">To open one, you will be asked for the last 4 digits of that person\'s Social Security number. We never store the digits.</p></div>';
    }
    function requestCard() {
      if (S.preview) return '';
      var rel = (V25().relationships || []).map(function (r) { return '<option>' + esc(r) + '</option>'; }).join('');
      return '<div class="card"><h3>Link an account you manage</h3><p class="small">If you handle someone else\'s tax matters, for example as personal representative of a loved one\'s estate, under a power of attorney, or as a parent or guardian, you can see their file from your own login.     }
    function careBanner() {
      var v = V25();
      if (!v.estate) return '';
      return '<div class="banner"><span class="pill gold">WITH CARE</span><span>We are sorry for your loss. We will take this one step at a time with you, and nothing is filed without your approval.</span></div>';
    }

    var _home25 = CV.home;

    var _rc25 = renderClient;

    ACT.factYes = function (el) { run(el, 'factAnswer', { id: el.dataset.id, answer: 'yes' }).then(function (b) { afterClient(b); }, function () {}); };
    ACT.factNo = function (el) { $$('form[data-form="factSend"][data-id="' + el.dataset.id + '"]').forEach(function (f) { f.style.display = ''; var t = f.querySelector('textarea'); if (t) t.focus(); }); };

      var code = '<div class="card"><h3>Access code (last 4 of their SSN)</h3><p class="small">' + (v.code.set ? 'Set ' + day(v.code.at) + (v.code.by ?     var _ed25 = editorV2;

    function factModal(f) {

    ACT.factEdit = function (el) { var v = (S.cur && S.cur.v25) || { facts: [] }; factModal(el.dataset.id ? findIn(v.facts, el.dataset.id) : null); };
    ACT.factDecide = function (el) {

      if ((el.dataset.k === 'remove' || el.dataset.k === 'decline') && !el.dataset.sure) { el.dataset.sure = '1'; el.textContent = 'Press again to confirm'; return; }
      var f = el.closest('form'), tgt = f && f.targetClientId ? f.targetClientId.value : '';
      run(el, 'repDecide', { id: el.dataset.id, decision: el.dataset.k, back: el.dataset.back || '', targetClientId: tgt }, 'adminok').then(afterAdmin, function () {});
    };
    FORM.factSave = function (f, d, btn) { d.id = f.dataset.id; d.clientId = S.cur.client.Id; run(btn, 'factSave', d, 'adminok').then(function (r) { closeModal(); afterAdmin(r); toast('Saved.'); }, function () {}); };

    var _qc25 = qCount;
    qCount = function () { var q = A().queue || {}; return _qc25() + (q.repRequests || []).length + (q.factChanges || []).length; };
    var _q25 = AV.queue;
    AV.queue = function () {
      var q = A().queue || {}, html = _q25(), cl = A().v25clients || [];
      var rr = q.repRequests || [], fc = q.factChanges || [];

        return '<div class="card"><div class="small muted">' + esc(l.RepName) + ' · ' + day(l.CreatedAt) + '</div><b>' + esc(l.RequestedName) + '</b> · ' + esc(l.Relationship) + '<div class="small">' + (l.MatchedName ?       }).join('');
      if (fc.length) sec += '<h2>Details sent by clients <span class="pill">' + fc.length + '</span></h2>' + fc.map(function (f) {
        return '<div class="card"><div class="small muted">' + esc(f.ClientName) + ' · ' + esc(f.AnsweredBy) + ' · ' + day(f.AnsweredAt) + '</div><b>' + esc(f.Label) + '</b><div class="small">On file: ' + (f.Value ? esc(f.Value) : '<span class="muted">(blank)</span>') + '</div><div>Client says: "' + esc(f.ClientAnswer) + '"</div>' + (f.FirmNote ? '<div class="small muted">Firm only: ' + esc(f.FirmNote) + '</div>' : '') + '<div class="row" style="gap:8px;margin-top:8px"><button class="btn sm navy" data-act="factDecide" data-k="accept" data-id="' + esc(f.Id) + '">Accept change</button><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="factDecide" data-k="keep" data-id="' + esc(f.Id) + '">Keep ours, ask again</button></div></div>';
      }).join('');
      return html.replace('<h1>Queue</h1>', '<h1>Queue</h1>' + sec);
    };
  } catch (e) { try { console.error('v2.5', e); } catch (x) {} }
})();

/* ===== AUTO PLAN & GOALS v2.6 (2026-10-27) — additive UI. Wraps editorV2 and propBody; nothing above is changed. ===== */
(function () {
  try {
    if (!CTABS.some(function (t) { return t[0] === 'autoplan'; })) { var ui = -1; CTABS.forEach(function (t, i) { if (t[0] === 'updates') ui = i; }); CTABS.splice(ui === -1 ? 3 : ui + 1, 0, ['autoplan', 'Auto plan']); }
    function apTab(d) {
      var a = d.ap || {}, cid = d.client.Id;
      if (a.error) return '<div class="card"><div class="err">' + esc(a.error) + '</div></div>';
      var stat = '<div class="card"><div class="row"><h3 style="flex:1 1 auto">Auto plan &amp; goals</h3><button class="btn sm navy" data-act="apRun" data-id="' + esc(cid) + '">Build plan &amp; goals now</button><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="apRunAll" data-id="' + esc(cid) + '">Run for all clients</button></div>' +
        '<p class="small">Builds this client\'s plan and the goals that give the best lawful outcome from everything on file: plan, net worth, documents read, messages, confirmed details and the transcripts and notes below. It also builds a plan for any goal the client set. <b>Everything waits in Plan updates for your approval.</b></p>' +
        '<div class="row" style="gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">' + (a.ai ? '<span class="pill good">AI drafting on</span>' : '<span class="pill warn">' + (a.aiKey ? 'AI off for this client (needs AI consent or AI firm use)' : 'No Claude API key — using the rule library') + '</span>') +
        (a.auto ? '<span class="pill good">Nightly run on</span>' : '<span class="pill">Nightly run off</span>') + (a.scan ? '<span class="pill good">Reads transcripts in the Drive folder</span>' : '') +
        (a.pending ? '<button class="pill gold" data-act="openClient" data-id="' + esc(cid) + '" data-tab="updates">' + a.pending + ' waiting in Plan updates</button>' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<form data-form="apSettings" style="margin-top:10px"><label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="auto"' + (a.auto ? ' checked' : '') + '> Run every night for clients whose information changed</label><label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="scan"' + (a.scan ? ' checked' : '') + '> Pick up Google Docs / text files named "transcript", "meeting", "notes" or "call" from client Drive folders</label><button class="btn sm ghost" type="submit">Save</button></form></div>';
      var add = '<div class="card"><h3>Add a transcript or conversation notes</h3><p class="small muted">Firm-only. Never shown to the client. Full SSN, card and bank numbers are masked before saving.</p><form data-form="apSource">' +
        '<div class="row"><div><label>Kind<select name="kind">' + (a.kinds || []).map(function (k) { return '<option>' + esc(k) + '</option>'; }).join('') + '</select></label></div><div><label>Title<input name="title" placeholder="e.g. Oct 26 Teams meeting"></label></div></div>' +
        '<label>Paste the transcript or notes<textarea name="text" rows="8" placeholder="Paste the meeting transcript, call notes or the client\'s email here"></textarea></label>' +
        '<label>Or upload a .txt / .vtt / .srt transcript<input type="file" id="apFile" accept=".txt,.vtt,.srt,.md,text/plain"></label>' +
        '<label class="check"><input type="checkbox" name="runNow" checked> Build plan &amp; goal updates from it right away</label><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Save</button></form></div>';
      var src = '<div class="card"><h3>Transcripts &amp; notes on file <span class="pill">' + (a.sources || []).length + '</span></h3>' + ((a.sources || []).length ? a.sources.map(function (s) {
        return '<details><summary><b>' + esc(s.Title) + '</b> <span class="pill">' + esc(s.Kind) + '</span> <span class="pill ' + (s.Status === 'new' ? 'gold' : '') + '">' + (s.Status === 'new' ? 'not used yet' : 'used') + '</span> <span class="small muted">' + day(s.CreatedAt) + ' · ' + esc(s.By) + ' · ' + s.Chars + ' chars</span></summary><div class="in"><div class="diff">' + esc(s.Preview) + (s.Chars > 280 ? '…' : '') + '</div>' +
          (s.FileId ? '<a class="btn sm ghost" target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="https://drive.google.com/open?id=' + esc(s.FileId) + '">Open in Drive</a> ' : '') + '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="apDel" data-id="' + esc(s.Id) + '">Remove</button></div></details>';
      }).join('') : '<p class="muted">None yet. Paste one above, or save a Google Doc with "transcript" in its name in the client\'s Drive folder.</p>') + '</div>';
      var runs = (a.runs || []).length ? '<div class="card"><h3>Recent runs</h3><table class="t"><tr><th>When</th><th>How</th><th>Updates</th><th>Note</th></tr>' + a.runs.map(function (r) { return '<tr><td>' + esc(String(r.At).replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 16)) + '</td><td>' + esc(r.Mode) + '</td><td>' + esc(r.Proposals) + '</td><td>' + esc(r.Note) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('') + '</table></div>' : '';
      return stat + add + src + runs;
    }
    var _edAP = editorV2;
    editorV2 = function (tab, d, body) { if (tab === 'autoplan') return apTab(d); return _edAP(tab, d, body); };

    var _pbAP = propBody;
    propBody = function (p) {
      if (p.Target !== 'Goals') return _pbAP(p);
      var a = p.after || {}, pl = a.plan || {}, lg = pl.legality || {};
      var ta = function (n, label, v, rows) { return '<label>' + label + '<textarea name="a_' + n + '" rows="' + (rows || 3) + '">' + esc(v || '') + '</textarea></label>'; };
      var inp = function (n, label, v) { return '<label>' + label + '<input name="a_' + n + '" value="' + esc(v || '') + '"></label>'; };
      return (p.Action === 'plan' ? '<p class="small"><span class="pill">Client\'s own goal</span> This adds the plan and steps to the goal the client set.</p>' : '<p class="small"><span class="pill gold">Suggested goal</span> Shows on the client\'s Goals tab after you apply it.</p>') +
        inp('Title', 'Goal', a.Title) + '<div class="row"><div>' + inp('Person', 'Person (blank = household)', a.Person) + '</div><div>' + inp('verdict', 'Is it allowed?', lg.verdict) + '</div></div>' +
        ta('Detail', 'Why this is the best outcome for them', a.Detail, 2) + ta('summary', 'How to reach it (client sees this)', pl.summary, 3) +
        ta('outcomesText', 'Outcomes (one per line, "Label: text")', (pl.outcomes || []).map(function (o) { return o.label + ': ' + o.text; }).join('\n'), 3) +
        inp('codes', 'Law cited (separate with ;)', (lg.codes || []).join('; ')) + (lg.explanation ? '<p class="small muted">' + esc(lg.explanation) + '</p>' : '');
    };

    ACT.apRun = function (el) { run(el, 'apRun', { clientId: el.dataset.id }, 'adminok').then(function (r) { afterAdmin(r); }, function () {}); };
    ACT.apRunAll = function (el) {
      if (!el.dataset.sure) { el.dataset.sure = '1'; el.textContent = 'Press again — runs every client (a few minutes)'; return; }
      run(el, 'apRunAll', { clientId: el.dataset.id }, 'adminok').then(function (r) { afterAdmin(r); }, function () {});
    };
    ACT.apDel = function (el) { if (!el.dataset.sure) { el.dataset.sure = '1'; el.textContent = 'Press again to remove'; return; } run(el, 'apSourceDelete', { id: el.dataset.id }, 'adminok').then(afterAdmin, function () {}); };
    FORM.apSettings = function (f, d, btn) { run(btn, 'apSettings', { clientId: S.cur.client.Id, auto: d.auto ? 'Y' : 'N', scan: d.scan ? 'Y' : 'N' }, 'adminok').then(afterAdmin, function () {}); };
    FORM.apSource = function (f, d, btn) {
      var payload = { clientId: S.cur.client.Id, kind: d.kind, title: d.title, text: d.text, runNow: !!d.runNow };
      var inp = $('#apFile'), go = function () { run(btn, 'apSourceAdd', payload, 'adminok').then(function (r) { f.reset(); afterAdmin(r); }, function () {}); };
      if (inp && inp.files && inp.files[0]) readFile(inp, 3000000).then(function (x) { payload.fileName = x.name; payload.mime = (x.dataUrl.match(/^data:([^;]+)/) || [])[1] || 'text/plain'; payload.fileB64 = String(x.dataUrl).split(',')[1]; go(); }, function (e) { toast(e.message); });
      else go();
    };
  } catch (e) { try { console.error('v2.6', e); } catch (x) {} }
})();
</script>

<script>
/* Team access v1.0 (Oct 27, 2026): "My apps" page for the owner and team logins. Additive; nothing above changed. */
(function () {
  if (typeof AVIEWS === 'undefined' || typeof AV === 'undefined') return;
  if (!AVIEWS.some(function (v) { return v[0] === 'myapps'; })) {
    var at = 0; for (var i = 0; i < AVIEWS.length; i++) if (AVIEWS[i][0] === 'clients') at = i + 1;
    AVIEWS.splice(at, 0, ['myapps', 'My apps']);
  }
  AVIEW_PERM.myapps = '';
  AV.myapps = function () {
    var me = ME(), d = me.apps || { apps: [], other: [] };
    var intro = me.owner
      ? '<p class="muted">This is the page your team sees. Each team login uses the <b>same email and PIN</b> in every app below. Reset a PIN or turn a login off on the Team page and it changes everywhere at once.</p>'
      : '<p class="muted">You use <b>one sign-in</b> for every app below: the same email and PIN you used to get here. If you forget your PIN, ask ' + esc(B.signAs || 'Cyars') + ' to reset it; the new PIN works everywhere at once. You only see the clients assigned to you.</p>';
    var cards = (d.apps || []).map(function (a) {
      var row = function (label, text) { return text ? '<p class="small" style="margin:6px 0"><b>' + esc(label) + ':</b> ' + esc(text) + '</p>' : ''; };
      return '<div class="card"><h3 style="margin:0 0 4px">' + esc(a.title) + '</h3><div class="small muted">' + esc(a.who) + '</div>' +
        row('What it is for', a.why) + row('What it does', a.does) + row('What you can do', a.can) + row('What waits for Cyars', a.held) + row('How to sign in', a.signin) +
        (a.url && a.url.indexOf('__') !== 0 ? '<div class="row" style="margin-top:8px"><a class="btn sm navy" href="' + esc(a.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Open ' + esc(a.title) + '</a></div>' : '<p class="small muted">Link coming soon.</p>') + '</div>';
    }).join('');
    var other = (d.other || []).map(function (t) { return '<tr><td><b>' + esc(t.title) + '</b></td><td>' + esc(t.why) + '</td><td>' + esc(t.signin) + '</td></tr>'; }).join('');
    return '<h1>My apps</h1>' + intro + '<div class="grid g2">' + cards + '</div>' +
      (other ? '<h2 style="margin-top:20px">Other tools (separate logins)</h2><div class="card"><table class="t"><tr><th>Tool</th><th>What it is for</th><th>Sign-in</th></tr>' + other + '</table></div>' : '');
  };
  try { if (S && S.role === 'admin' && S.admin) renderAdmin(); } catch (e) {}
})();
</script>
<script>
/* v3.1 Growth & Deadlines (Oct 28, 2026): Payments (pay now with informed consent), specials + referral sender,
   goal tracker, Deadline Guard. Additive; nothing above changed. Server side: Growth.gs. */
(function () {
  try {
    function G() { var b = C(); return (b && b.growth) || { payments: [], specials: [], dates: [] }; }
    function payBox(kind, id, text, label) {
      return '<form data-form="gPay" data-k="' + kind + '" data-id="' + esc(id) + '" style="margin-top:10px">' +
        '<p class="small" style="background:#F6F1E4;padding:10px;border-radius:6px"><b>What you are agreeing to:</b> ' + esc(text) + '</p>' +

        '<button class="btn navy" type="submit">' + esc(label) + '</button><div class="gout" role="status"></div></form>';
    }
    CV.pay = function () {
      var g = G(), due = g.payments.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'due' || p.status === 'clicked'; }), done = g.payments.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'paid'; });
      var ro = !!S.preview;
      var dueHtml = due.length ? due.map(function (p) {
        return '<div class="card"><h3 style="margin:0">' + esc(p.title) + '</h3><p style="font-size:1.4rem;margin:6px 0"><b>' + money(p.amount) + '</b></p>' +
          (p.status === 'clicked' ? '<p class="small"><span class="pill gold">Approved ' + day(p.consentAt) + '</span> If you have not finished paying, approve again below to reopen the payment page.</p>' : '') +
          (ro ? '<p class="small muted">Preview: the client signs and pays here.</p>' : payBox('pay', p.id, p.consentText, 'Agree and pay now')) + '</div>';
      }).join('') : '<p class="muted">Nothing to pay right now.</p>';
      var sp = g.specials.length ? '<h2 style="margin-top:22px">Services and specials</h2><div class="grid g2">' + g.specials.map(function (s) {

          (ro ? '' : '<details><summary class="btn sm ghost" style="display:inline-block">Book and pay</summary>' + payBox('special', s.id, s.consentText, 'Agree and pay now') + '</details>') + '</div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>' : '';
      var hist = done.length ? '<div class="card" style="margin-top:18px"><h3>Paid</h3><div class="scroll"><table class="t"><tr><th>For</th><th>Amount</th><th>Paid</th><th>Your approval</th></tr>' + done.map(function (p) {
        return '<tr><td>' + esc(p.title) + '</td><td>' + money(p.amount) + '</td><td>' + day(p.paidAt) + '</td><td>' + (p.consentAt ? 'Signed by ' + esc(p.consentName) + ' on ' + day(p.consentAt) + ' <details><summary class="small">Read</summary><p class="small">' + esc(p.consentText) + '</p></details>' : '-') + '</td></tr>';
      }).join('') + '</table></div></div>' : '';
      return '<h1>Payments</h1><p class="muted">Each payment shows exactly what it covers. You approve it here, then pay on the QuickBooks secure page. A dated copy of your approval stays in your file.</p>' + dueHtml + sp + hist;
    };
    FORM.gPay = function (f, d, btn) {
      var out = $('.gout', f);
      run(btn, f.dataset.k === 'special' ? 'gSpecialStart' : 'gPayStart', { id: f.dataset.id, agree: !!d.agree, name: d.name }).then(function (r) {
        if (r && /^https:\/\//.test(r.url)) out.innerHTML = '<p><a class="btn gold" href="' + esc(r.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Tap here to pay on QuickBooks</a></p><p class="small">Your approval is saved. The payment page opens in a new tab.</p>';
      }, function (e) { out.innerHTML = '<div class="err">' + esc(e.message) + '</div>'; });
    };
    var _home31 = CV.home;
    CV.home = function () {
      var h = _home31.apply(this, arguments), g = G(), extra = '';
      var due = g.payments.filter(function (p) { return p.status === 'due'; });

' <span class="pill warn">' + (x.days <= 0 ? 'today' : x.days + ' days') + '</span>' : '') + '</li>'; }).join('') + '</ul></div>';
      if (due.length) extra += '<div class="card"><h3>Waiting for your approval</h3><p>' + due.length + ' payment' + (due.length > 1 ? 's' : '') + ' ready.</p><button class="btn navy" data-act="cview" data-k="pay">Open Payments</button></div>';
      return extra ? '<div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:12px">' + extra + '</div>' + h : h;
    };
    ACT.cview = ACT.cview || function (el) { S.view = el.dataset.k; renderClient(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); };
    var _refer31 = CV.refer;
    CV.refer = function () {
      var h = _refer31.apply(this, arguments), g = G();

      return h + '<div class="card"><h3>Share a special</h3><p class="small muted">Send one of these links to a friend. It carries your code so we know you sent them.</p>' + g.specials.map(function (s, i) {

    };
    ACT.gCopy = function (el) { var i = document.getElementById(el.dataset.t); if (!i) return; i.select(); try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(i.value).then(function () { toast('Link copied.'); }, function () { document.execCommand('copy'); toast('Link copied.'); }); } catch (e) { document.execCommand('copy'); toast('Link copied.'); } };
    var _rc31 = renderClient;

      var g = G(), want = !!(g.payments.length || g.specials.length), at = -1;
      CVIEWS.forEach(function (v, i) { if (v[0] === 'pay') at = i; });
      if (want && at === -1) { var pos = -1; CVIEWS.forEach(function (v, i) { if (v[0] === 'tasks') pos = i; }); CVIEWS.splice(pos === -1 ? 1 : pos + 1, 0, ['pay', 'Payments']); }
      if (!want && at !== -1) { CVIEWS.splice(at, 1); if (S.view === 'pay') S.view = 'home'; }

    };

    function gDeadHtml(g) {

    FORM.gGoal = function (f, d, btn) { run(btn, 'gGoalSave', d, 'adminok').then(function (r) { gSet(r); toast('Saved.'); }, function () {}); };
    FORM.gSend = function (f, d, btn) { if (!confirmList(d.list)) return; run(btn, 'gRefSend', d, 'adminok').then(function (r) { gSet(r); toast('Done. See the results under Send.'); }, function () {}); };
    function confirmList(l) { var n = String(l || '').split(/[\n,;]+/).filter(function (x) { return x.trim(); }).length; return n ? window.confirm('Send to ' + n + ' contact' + (n > 1 ? 's' : '') + '? Emails go out right away.') : (toast('Paste at least one email or phone.'), false); }
    FORM.gSpecial = function (f, d, btn) { d.id = f.dataset.id; run(btn, 'gSpecialSave', d, 'adminok').then(function (r) { gSet(r); toast('Special saved.'); }, function () {}); };
    FORM.gPayNew = function (f, d, btn) { run(btn, 'gPaymentNew', d, 'adminok').then(function (r) { gSet(r); toast('Added. The client sees it under Payments.'); }, function () {}); };
    FORM.gDead = function (f, d, btn) { run(btn, 'gDeadlineSave', d, 'adminok').then(function (r) { gSet(r); toast('Deadline added.'); }, function () {}); };
    ACT.gMark = function (el) { var s = el.dataset.s; if (s === 'void' && !window.confirm('Cancel this payment request?')) return; run(el, 'gPaymentMark', { id: el.dataset.id, status: s }, 'adminok').then(gSet, function () {}); };
    ACT.gDone = function (el) { run(el, 'gDeadlineDone', { id: el.dataset.id, reopen: !!el.dataset.r }, 'adminok').then(gSet, function () {}); };
    ACT.gRun = function (el) { run(el, 'gDeadlineRun', {}, 'adminok').then(gSet, function () {}); };
    try { if (S && S.role === 'admin' && S.admin) renderAdmin(); } catch (e) {}
  } catch (e) {}
})();
</script>

<script>
/* v3.3 Suggestions & Notices (Nov 6, 2026): tap a client, pick the kind of notice, say a few words,
   get an in-depth write-up, send to the client's portal + email. Additive; nothing above changed.
   Server side: Suggestions.gs. */
(function () {
  try {
    /* ---------------- shared ---------------- */
    function SG() { var b = C(); return (b && b.suggestions) || []; }
    function rich(t) {
      var out = [], list = false, para = [];
      function flushP() { if (para.length) { out.push('<p>' + para.join(' ') + '</p>'); para = []; } }
      function flushL() { if (list) { out.push('</ul>'); list = false; } }
      function inline(s) { return esc(s).replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, '<b>$1</b>'); }
      String(t || '').split(/\n/).forEach(function (line) {
        var l = line.trim();
        if (!l) { flushP(); flushL(); return; }
        if (/^#{1,3}\s+/.test(l)) { flushP(); flushL(); out.push('<h3 style="margin:18px 0 6px">' + inline(l.replace(/^#{1,3}\s+/, '')) + '</h3>'); return; }
        if (/^[-*\u2022]\s+/.test(l) || /^\d+[.)]\s+/.test(l)) { flushP(); if (!list) { out.push('<ul class="pts">'); list = true; } out.push('<li>' + inline(l.replace(/^([-*\u2022]|\d+[.)])\s+/, '')) + '</li>'); return; }
        flushL(); para.push(inline(l));
      });
      flushP(); flushL();
      return out.join('');
    }
    var TYPE_ICON = { idea: '\u{1F4A1}', save: '\u{1F4B0}', action: '\u2757', docs: '\u{1F4C4}', deadline: '\u23f0', opportunity: '\u2b50', plan: '\u{1F5FA}', note: '\u2709' };
    function prioPill(k, label) { return '<span class="pill ' + (k === 'action' ? 'bad' : k === 'recommended' ? 'gold' : '') + '">' + esc(label || k) + '</span>'; }
    function linksHtml(links) {
      if (!links || !links.length) return '';
      return '<div class="card" style="margin-top:12px"><h3 style="margin-top:0">Links</h3>' + links.map(function (l) {
        if (l.go) return '<div style="margin:6px 0"><button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="sGo" data-k="' + esc(l.go) + '">' + esc(l.label) + '</button></div>';
        return '<div style="margin:6px 0"><a href="' + esc(l.url) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' + esc(l.label) + '</a></div>';
      }).join('') + '</div>';
    }

    /* ---------------- deep links from the email (?go=sugg&sid=...) ---------------- */
    S.goView = ''; S.goSid = '';

    /* ---------------- client side ---------------- */
    CV.sugg = function () {
      var list = SG(), ro = !!S.preview;
      if (S.sgOpen) {
        var x = list.filter(function (r) { return r.id === S.sgOpen; })[0];
        if (x) {
          var ans = x.response ? '<span class="small">' + esc(x.responseText) + '</span>' : '') + '</div>' : '';
          var form = ro ? '<p class="small muted">Preview: the client answers here.</p>' :
            '<form data-form="sRespond" data-id="' + esc(x.id) + '" class="card"><h3 style="margin-top:0">' + (x.response ? 'Change your answer' : 'What do you think?') + '</h3>' +
            '<input type="hidden" name="response" value="' + esc(x.response || '') + '"><div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:6px 0 12px">' + [['interested', 'I am interested'], ['notnow', 'Not now'], ['question', 'I have a question']].map(function (o) {
              var on = x.response === o[0]; return '<button type="button" class="btn ' + (on ? 'gold' : 'ghost') + '" data-act="sAns" data-v="' + o[0] + '">' + o[1] + '</button>'; }).join('') + '</div>' +
            '<label>Anything you want to add (optional)<textarea name="text" rows="3" maxlength="3000"></textarea></label><button class="btn navy" type="submit">Send my answer</button></form>';
          return '<button class="btn sm ghost" data-act="sBack">&larr; All suggestions</button>' +
            '<div class="card" style="margin-top:10px"><div class="small muted">' + (TYPE_ICON[x.type] || '') + ' ' + esc(x.typeLabel) + ' \u00b7 ' + day(x.sentAt) + ' ' + prioPill(x.priority, x.priorityLabel) + '</div>' +
            '<h1 style="margin:8px 0">' + esc(x.title) + '</h1>' + (x.brief ? '<p style="font-size:1.08rem"><b>' + esc(x.brief) + '</b></p>' : '') + rich(x.detail) + '</div>' +
            linksHtml(x.links) + ans + form +
            '<p class="small muted">This is a suggestion from ' + esc(B.preparer || 'your preparer') + ' for you to think about. Nothing changes until you decide. Questions? Use Messages or call ' + esc(B.phone || '') + '.</p>';
        }
        S.sgOpen = '';
      }
      if (!list.length) return '<h1>Suggestions</h1><p class="muted">Nothing here yet. New ideas and notices from ' + esc(B.preparer || 'us') + ' will show up here.</p>';
      return '<h1>Suggestions</h1><p class="muted">Ideas, notices and next steps from ' + esc(B.preparer || 'us') + '. Tap one to read it in full.</p>' + list.map(function (x) {
        return '<button class="card" data-act="sOpen" data-id="' + esc(x.id) + '" style="display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;cursor:pointer;border-left:5px solid ' + (x.readAt ? 'var(--line)' : 'var(--gold)') + '">' +
          '<div class="small muted">' + (TYPE_ICON[x.type] || '') + ' ' + esc(x.typeLabel) + ' \u00b7 ' + day(x.sentAt) + ' ' + prioPill(x.priority, x.priorityLabel) + (x.readAt ? '' : ' <span class="pill gold">New</span>') + (x.response ? ' <span class="pill good">Answered</span>' : '') + '</div>' +
          '<h3 style="margin:6px 0">' + esc(x.title) + '</h3><p class="small" style="margin:0">' + esc(x.brief) + '</p></button>';
      }).join('');
    };
    ACT.sOpen = function (el) {

    };
    function sNavLabel() { var n = SG().filter(function (x) { return !x.readAt; }).length, b = document.querySelector('nav button[data-k="sugg"]'); if (b) b.textContent = 'Suggestions' + (n ?     };

      else if (want && at !== -1) CVIEWS[at][1] = lab;

      }

    };

    }

It does not read this client\'s file because they have not signed the \u00a77216 AI consent.') + ' Nothing goes to the client until you press Send.</p>' +
            '<button class="btn navy" type="submit">Write it up</button></form>' +
            '<div class="card"><h3 style="margin-top:0">For ' + esc(cc.name) + '</h3>' + (mine.length ? mine.map(function (x) { return itemRow(x, false); }).join('') : '<p class="muted">No suggestions yet.</p>') + '</div>';
        }
        S.sgClient = '';
      }
      /* ---- 1. pick a client ---- */
      var drafts = items.filter(function (r) { return r.status === 'draft'; }), answers = items.filter(function (r) { return r.response; }).slice(0, 10);
      return '<h1>Suggest</h1><p class="muted">Tap a client, pick the kind of notice, say a few words. You review the write-up before anything is sent.</p>' +
        '<input id="sgFind" placeholder="Find a client" value="' + esc(S.sgFind) + '" style="width:100%;margin-bottom:10px" autocomplete="off">' +
        '<div class="grid g3">' + (g.clients || []).map(function (c) {
          var q = String(c.name || '').toLowerCase(), hide = S.sgFind && q.indexOf(S.sgFind.toLowerCase()) === -1;
          return '<button class="card" data-act="sPick" data-id="' + esc(c.id) + '" data-sgname="' + esc(q) + '" style="text-align:left;cursor:pointer;margin:0;width:100%' + (hide ? ';display:none' : '') + '"><b>' + esc(c.name) + '</b>' +
            '<div class="small muted">' + esc(String(c.occupation || '').slice(0, 70)) + '</div><div class="small" style="margin-top:6px">' + (c.emails ? '\u2709 email on file' : '<span style="color:var(--warn)">no email \u2014 portal only</span>') +
            (c.drafts ? ' \u00b7 <span class="pill warn">' + c.drafts + ' draft</span>' : '') + (c.sent ? ' \u00b7 ' + c.sent + ' sent' : '') + (c.answered ? ' \u00b7 <span class="pill gold">' + c.answered + ' answered</span>' : '') + '</div></button>';
        }).join('') + '</div>' +
        (drafts.length ? '<div class="card"><h3 style="margin-top:0">Drafts waiting for you</h3>' + drafts.map(function (x) { return itemRow(x, true); }).join('') + '</div>' : '') +
        (answers.length ? '<div class="card"><h3 style="margin-top:0">Latest answers from clients</h3>' + answers.map(function (x) { return itemRow(x, true); }).join('') + '</div>' : '');
    }
    ACT.sPick = function (el) { S.sgClient = el.dataset.id; S.sgDraft = ''; sPaint(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); };
    ACT.sClients = function () { S.sgClient = ''; S.sgDraft = ''; sPaint(); };
    ACT.sBackList = function (el) { S.sgDraft = ''; S.sgClient = el.dataset.c; S.sgPrev = false; sPaint(); };
    ACT.sEdit = function (el) { S.sgDraft = el.dataset.id; S.sgClient = el.dataset.c; S.sgPrev = false; if (S.aview !== 'suggest') { S.aview = 'suggest'; S.cur = null; renderAdmin(); } else { sPaint(); } window.scrollTo(0, 0); };
    ACT.sPrev = function () { S.sgPrev = !S.sgPrev; sPaint(); };
    ACT.sDel = function (el) {
      if (el.dataset.armed !== '1') { el.dataset.armed = '1'; el.textContent = 'Tap again to remove'; return; }
      run(el, 'sDelete', { id: el.dataset.id }, 'adminok').then(function (d) { S.sgDraft = ''; sSet(d); }, function () {});
    };
    ACT.sSend = function (el) {
      if (el.dataset.armed !== '1') { el.dataset.armed = '1'; el.textContent = 'Tap again to confirm send'; return; }
      run(el, 'sSend', { id: el.dataset.id, resend: !!el.dataset.resend }, 'adminok').then(sSet, function () { el.dataset.armed = ''; });
    };
    FORM.sBuild = function (f, d, btn) {
      var gos = []; Object.keys(d).forEach(function (k) { if (k.indexOf('go_') === 0 && d[k]) gos.push('go:' + k.slice(3)); });
      var t = f.querySelector('input[name=type]:checked'), p = f.querySelector('input[name=priority]:checked');
      S.sgType = t ? t.value : 'note'; S.sgPrio = p ? p.value : 'recommended';
      var links = String(d.links || '').trim(); links = (links ? links + '\n' : '') + gos.join('\n');
      run(btn, 'sBuild', { clientId: d.clientId, type: S.sgType, priority: S.sgPrio, person: d.person || '', note: d.note, links: links }, 'adminok').then(function (r) { S.sgPrev = true; sSet(r); window.scrollTo(0, 0); }, function () {});
    };
    FORM.sSave = function (f, d, btn) {
      var t = f.querySelector('input[name=type]:checked'), p = f.querySelector('input[name=priority]:checked');
      run(btn, 'sSave', { id: f.dataset.id, title: d.title, brief: d.brief, detail: d.detail, links: d.links, person: d.person || '', type: t ? t.value : undefined, priority: p ? p.value : undefined }, 'adminok').then(sSet, function () {});
    };

    /* ---- "Suggestions" tab inside each client's file ---- */
    if (!CTABS.some(function (t) { return t[0] === 'suggest'; })) { var mi = -1; CTABS.forEach(function (t, i) { if (t[0] === 'messages') mi = i; }); CTABS.splice(mi === -1 ? CTABS.length : mi + 1, 0, ['suggest', 'Suggestions']); }
    var _ev33 = editorV2;
    editorV2 = function (tab, d, body) {
      if (tab !== 'suggest') return _ev33.apply(this, arguments);
      var cid = d.client.Id;
      if (!ME().owner) return '<div class="card"><p class="muted">Only the owner login sends suggestions.</p></div>';
      var mine = S.sg ? (S.sg.items || []).filter(function (x) { return x.clientId === cid; }) : null;
      if (!S.sg) sLoad(function () { if (S.cur && S.ctab === 'suggest') renderAdmin(); }).catch(function () {});
      return '<div class="card"><div class="row"><h3 style="flex:1 1 auto;margin:0">Suggestions & notices</h3><button class="btn navy" data-act="sNewFor" data-id="' + esc(cid) + '">New suggestion</button></div>' +
        (mine === null ? '<p class="muted">Loading\u2026</p>' : mine.length ? mine.map(function (x) { return itemRow(x, false); }).join('') : '<p class="muted">None yet.</p>') + '</div>';
    };
    ACT.sNewFor = function (el) { S.sgClient = el.dataset.id; S.sgDraft = ''; S.aview = 'suggest'; S.cur = null; renderAdmin(); window.scrollTo(0, 0); };
    try { if (S && S.role === 'admin' && S.admin) renderAdmin(); } catch (e) {}
  } catch (e) { try { console.log('v3.3 suggestions UI failed: ' + e.message); } catch (x) {} }
})();

<script>

   Server side: DeviceLogin.gs. */
(function () {

    function devGet() { try { var v = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cc_dev') || 'null'); return v && v.id && v.key ? v : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
    function devSet(v) { try { if (v) localStorage.setItem('cc_dev', JSON.stringify(v)); else localStorage.removeItem('cc_dev'); } catch (e) {} }
    function devLabel() { var u = navigator.userAgent || ''; var os = /iPhone|iPad/.test(u) ? 'iPhone/iPad' : /Android/.test(u) ? 'Android' : /Mac/.test(u) ? 'Mac' : /Windows/.test(u) ? 'Windows' : 'Device'; var br = /Edg\//.test(u) ? 'Edge' : /Chrome\//.test(u) ? 'Chrome' : /Safari\//.test(u) ? 'Safari' : /Firefox\//.test(u) ? 'Firefox' : 'browser'; return os + ' \u00b7 ' + br; }
    function until(days) { var d = new Date(Date.now() + days * 86400000); return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' }); }

    /* ---- "Keep me signed in" box on the login forms ---- */
    var _lm34 = loginModal;
    loginModal = function (tab) {
      var r = _lm34.apply(this, arguments), m = document.getElementById('modal');

          var f = m.querySelector('form[data-form="' + x[0] + '"]'); if (!f || f.querySelector('input[name=remember]')) return;
          var b = f.querySelector('button[type=submit]'); if (!b) return;
          var l = document.createElement('label'); l.className = 'check'; l.style.margin = '8px 0 12px';
          l.innerHTML = '<input type="checkbox" name="remember"' + (x[1] ? ' checked' : '') + '> <span>' + x[2] + '</span>';
